## internship-platform/README.md

````markdown
# InternshipRadar AI

## 1. Project Overview and Objectives
InternshipRadar AI is a production-grade web application designed to periodically collect PFE (End-of-Studies) and internship listings from permitted websites across Europe, focusing specifically on:
* Artificial Intelligence, Machine Learning, and Deep Learning
* Data Science and Data Engineering
* NLP and Large Language Models (LLMs)
* Computer Vision and Applied AI Research

The system automatically detects new offers, deduplicates records, matches them against student preferences using a deterministic ranking engine, and dispatches email alerts.

## 2. Architecture Diagram
```mermaid
graph TD
    Client[Next.js Frontend / Admin UI] -->|HTTPS / REST API| API[FastAPI Backend]
    
    subgraph Storage & Caching
        DB[(PostgreSQL Database)]
        Redis[(Redis Cache & Task Broker)]
    end

    subgraph Async Background Engine
        Beat[Celery Beat Scheduler] -->|Enqueues Scheduled Tasks| Redis
        Worker[Celery Workers] <-->|Processes Jobs / Fetches Tasks| Redis
        Worker -->|Reads / Writes Offers & Logs| DB
        Worker -->|Sends Notifications| EmailService[Email Service Interface]
        API -->|Triggers Manual Scraping / Jobs| Redis
    end

    subgraph Scraping Infrastructure
        Worker -->|Executes Spiders| Scrapy[Scrapy Framework Engine]
        Scrapy -->|Rate-Limited / Respectful Requests| Targets[Target Websites / APIs / RSS]
    end

    API <--> DB
    API <--> Redis
    EmailService -->|Delivers Alerts| UserEmail[Subscriber Inbox]

    3. Technology Stack and RationaleFrontend: Next.js 14, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS (Modern, responsive, fast server-side rendering).Backend REST API: FastAPI (High-performance asynchronous Python API with automatic Swagger/OpenAPI docs).Scraping Subsystem: Scrapy (Robust, rate-limited web crawling framework with built-in pipeline support).Database & ORM: PostgreSQL 15 & SQLAlchemy 2.x with Alembic migrations (Reliable relational data integrity and structured schema evolution).Background Processing: Celery & Redis (Scalable asynchronous task distribution and distributed locking).Scheduling: Celery Beat (Authoritative single-clock periodic task scheduler).Email Provider Interface: Interchangeable adapter supporting SMTP, Resend, and a Development Console fallback.4. Repository StructurePlaintextinternship-platform/
├── backend/                  # FastAPI application, models, schemas, tasks, migrations
│   ├── app/
│   │   ├── api/v1/           # REST endpoints (auth, offers, preferences, admin)
│   │   ├── core/             # Config, database session, celery app, redis locks
│   │   ├── models/           # SQLAlchemy ORM models
│   │   ├── services/         # Ingestion, matching, and email provider services
│   │   └── tasks/            # Celery scraping, matching, and notification tasks
│   ├── alembic/              # Database migration scripts
│   └── tests/                # Pytest test suite
├── scraper/                  # Scrapy project, spiders, items, pipelines, and offline fixtures
│   └── internship_scraper/
│       ├── spiders/          # Source-specific feed/HTML spiders
│       └── tests/            # Fixture-based unit tests
├── frontend/                 # Next.js TypeScript application
│   └── src/app/              # Pages, components, and layout views
├── docker-compose.yml        # Infrastructure service orchestration
├── .env.example              # Environment variable template
└── README.md                 # Project documentation
5. PrerequisitesDocker and Docker Compose (v2.0+)Python 3.11+ (if running tests locally outside containers)Node.js 18+ (if running frontend locally outside containers)6. Environment ConfigurationCopy the template and configure your local settings:Bashcp .env.example .env
7. Installation and Startup CommandsLaunch all containerized services:Bashdocker compose up -d --build
8. Database Migration InstructionsApply pending Alembic migrations inside the backend container:Bashdocker compose exec backend alembic upgrade head
9. How to Run TestsExecute the full pytest suite inside the backend container:Bashdocker compose exec backend pytest
10. How to Configure a Permitted SourceAdministrators can register or update scraping sources via the database seed script or the /api/v1/admin/sources REST endpoint. Spiders strictly respect robots.txt and download delays.11. How to Run a Scrape ManuallyTrigger an out-of-band scrape via the Celery task runner or admin API:Bashdocker compose exec backend python -c "from app.tasks.scraping_tasks import scrape_source_task; scrape_source_task.delay(1)"
12. How to Configure Email NotificationsSet EMAIL_PROVIDER=resend or EMAIL_PROVIDER=smtp in your .env file along with credentials. In development, leave it unconfigured to default to the safe Console Provider (no real emails sent).13. How to Create a Subscriber and PreferencesUse the /api/v1/auth/register endpoint or the database seed script (python -m app.db.seed) to create a subscriber account and default preference profile.14. How to Inspect Logs and Troubleshoot ErrorsView backend API logs: docker compose logs -f backendView worker execution logs: docker compose logs -f workerView scheduler ticks: docker compose logs -f beat15. Known Limitations and Future ImprovementsLive scraping sources require ongoing maintenance due to target DOM changes; current testing relies heavily on RSS feeds and offline XML fixtures.Advanced semantic embedding matching is architected via an optional interface but defaults to a deterministic keyword/skill scoring engine.
---

## 2. Environment Configuration (`.env.example`)

```env
# Application Settings
PROJECT_NAME=InternshipRadar AI
ENVIRONMENT=development
DEBUG=true

# PostgreSQL Database
POSTGRES_USER=postgres
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=postgres
POSTGRES_DB=internship_radar
POSTGRES_SERVER=postgres
DATABASE_URL=postgresql+psycopg2://postgres:postgres@postgres:5432/internship_radar

# Redis & Celery
REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/0

# Security / JWT
SECRET_KEY=super-secret-development-key-change-in-production
ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES=11520

# Email Provider Configuration (console, smtp, or resend)
EMAIL_PROVIDER=console
SMTP_HOST=smtp.example.com
SMTP_PORT=587
SMTP_USER=user@example.com
SMTP_PASSWORD=secret
EMAILS_FROM_EMAIL=alerts@internshipradar.ai
RESEND_API_KEY=re_placeholder_key
3. Academic Project Report Outline & DiagramsReport OutlineIntroduction and Problem Statement: Context of PFE/end-of-studies internship hunting in Europe for AI/Data Science students; fragmentation of job boards.Functional and Non-Functional Requirements: User discovery, automated scraping, preference matching, notification workflows, scalability, and rate-limiting compliance.System Architecture: Monorepo containerization using Docker Compose, FastAPI, Next.js, Scrapy, Celery, Redis, and PostgreSQL.Database Design and Entity Relationship Diagram (ERD): Relational schema design across Users, Preferences, InternshipOffers, ScrapingSources, ScrapingRuns, and OfferMatches.Scraping Methodology and Source Selection: Ethical scraping practices, robots.txt adherence, feed parsing, and source adapters.New-Offer Detection and Deduplication: Canonical URL checking and SHA256 content fingerprinting to ensure idempotent ingestion.Subscriber Matching and Email Notification Workflow: Deterministic relevance scoring engine and abstracted email provider queueing.Implementation Details: Code modularity, database session management, and background worker safety.Testing Strategy and Actual Results: Unit tests, fixture-based spider tests, and workflow idempotency test results.Ethical, Legal, and Privacy Considerations: Terms of service compliance, avoiding CAPTCHA bypass, and data minimization.Limitations and Future Work: DOM volatility and optional LLM semantic embedding enhancements.Conclusion: Summary of achievements and project impact.Mermaid Diagrams1. System Architecture DiagramCode snippetgraph TD
    Client[Next.js Frontend] -->|REST API| API[FastAPI Backend]
    API --> DB[(PostgreSQL)]
    API --> Redis[(Redis Broker)]
    Beat[Celery Beat] -->|Schedules| Redis
    Worker[Celery Workers] <--> Redis
    Worker --> DB
    Worker --> Scrapy[Scrapy Engine]
    Scrapy --> External[Permitted Feeds / Sites]
2. Database Relationship Diagram (ERD)Code snippeterDiagram
    USERS ||--o{ PREFERENCES : has
    USERS ||--o{ OFFER_MATCHES : receives
    SCRAPING_SOURCES ||--o{ INTERNSHIP_OFFERS : produces
    SCRAPING_SOURCES ||--o{ SCRAPING_RUNS : logs
    INTERNSHIP_OFFERS ||--o{ OFFER_MATCHES : matches
    
    USERS {
        int id PK
        string email
        string hashed_password
        string role
    }
    PREFERENCES {
        int id PK
        int user_id FK
        json preferred_countries
        json target_roles
        json skills
    }
    INTERNSHIP_OFFERS {
        int id PK
        int source_id FK
        string canonical_url
        string fingerprint
        string title
        string company
        datetime first_seen_at
        datetime last_seen_at
    }
    OFFER_MATCHES {
        int id PK
        int user_id FK
        int offer_id FK
        float score
        json breakdown
    }
    SCRAPING_SOURCES {
        int id PK
        string name
        string slug
        boolean is_active
    }
    SCRAPING_RUNS {
        int id PK
        int source_id FK
        string status
        int items_found
    }
3. New-Offer Notification Sequence DiagramCode snippetsequenceDiagram
    participant Beat as Celery Beat
    participant Worker as Celery Worker
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant Matcher as Matching Engine
    participant Email as Email Provider

    Beat->>Worker: Dispatch scraping task
    Worker->>DB: Upsert offer (Idempotent check)
    alt Is New Offer
        DB-->>Worker: Return new offer ID
        Worker->>Matcher: Trigger match evaluation
        Matcher->>DB: Query subscriber preferences
        Matcher->>DB: Create OfferMatch record
        Matcher->>Email: Queue email notification task
        Email-->>Subscriber: Deliver alert (Console/SMTP/Resend)
    else Is Existing / Unchanged
        DB-->>Worker: Update last_seen_at timestamp only
        Note over Worker,Email: No duplicate match or notification created
    end
4. Final Acceptance ChecklistComponent / FeatureStatusVerification MethodDocker Compose OrchestrationImplemented & Testeddocker compose up -d starts all 6 services successfully.PostgreSQL & Alembic MigrationsImplemented & Testedalembic upgrade head applies schemas cleanly.Scrapy Scraping SubsystemImplemented & TestedVerified via offline XML fixtures and feed spiders.Idempotent Ingestion & DeduplicationImplemented & TestedSHA256 fingerprinting prevents duplicate records and alerts.Deterministic Matching EngineImplemented & TestedPytest suite validates skill overlap, PFE constraints, and scoring.Celery Background Workers & BeatImplemented & TestedDistributed Redis locking prevents scraping overlaps.Interchangeable Email ProviderImplemented & TestedSafe console fallback logs emails in development without external API keys.Next.js Frontend & REST APIImplemented (Partially Tested)FastAPI endpoints documented in Swagger (/docs); frontend UI scaffolded.5. Clean-Start & University Presentation Demonstration ScriptFollow this script step-by-step during your presentation to demonstrate the system live:Bash# Step 1: Clean start (stop and remove existing containers & volumes)
docker compose down -v

# Step 2: Build and launch all infrastructure services
docker compose up -d --build

# Step 3: Verify container health and running status
docker compose ps

# Step 4: Run database migrations
docker compose exec backend alembic upgrade head

# Step 5: Seed database with synthetic development data and subscribers
docker compose exec backend python -m app.db.seed

# Step 6: Run the automated test suite to prove system correctness
docker compose exec backend pytest -v

# Step 7: Open Swagger API Documentation in your browser
# Navigate to: http://localhost:8000/docs
````

## internship-platform/backend/Dockerfile

```
FROM python:3.11-slim

WORKDIR /app

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    build-essential libpq-dev curl \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY requirements.txt ./
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --upgrade pip \
    && pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

COPY . .

EXPOSE 8000

CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000", "--reload"]
```

## internship-platform/backend/alembic/env.py

```python
from logging.config import fileConfig
from sqlalchemy import engine_from_config, pool
from alembic import context
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__)))

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import Base
import app.models  # Ensures all ORM models (User, Preference, Offer, ScrapingSource, etc.) are registered

config = context.config
if config.config_file_name:
    fileConfig(config.config_file_name)

target_metadata = Base.metadata

def run_migrations_online():
    configuration = config.get_section(config.config_ini_section)
    configuration["sqlalchemy.url"] = settings.DATABASE_URL
    connectable = engine_from_config(
        configuration,
        prefix="sqlalchemy.",
        poolclass=pool.NullPool,
    )

    with connectable.connect() as connection:
        context.configure(connection=connection, target_metadata=target_metadata)
        with context.begin_transaction():
            context.run_migrations()

if context.is_offline_mode():
    context.configure(url=settings.DATABASE_URL, target_metadata=target_metadata)
    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()
else:
    run_migrations_online()
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/api/deps.py

```python
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from jose import jwt, JWTError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.user import User, UserRole

oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl=f"{settings.API_V1_STR}/auth/login")

def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> User:
    credentials_exception = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail="Could not validate credentials",
        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    )
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithms=["HS256"])
        user_id = int(payload.get("sub"))
    except (JWTError, ValueError, TypeError):
        raise credentials_exception

    user = db.get(User, user_id)
    if user is None or not user.is_active:
        raise credentials_exception
    return user

def get_current_admin(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)) -> User:
    """Strict server-side enforcement for administrator endpoints."""
    if current_user.role != UserRole.ADMIN.value:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, 
            detail="The user doesn't have enough privileges"
        )
    return current_user
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/api/v1/admin.py

```python
import ipaddress
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, joinedload

from app.api.deps import get_current_admin
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.models.notification import NotificationLog
from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer
from app.models.scraping import ScrapingRun, ScrapingSource
from app.models.user import User
from app.tasks.scraping_tasks import scrape_source_task

router = APIRouter(dependencies=[Depends(get_current_admin)])


def validate_safe_url(url: str):
    """Prevents administrators from configuring internal network scanning."""
    parsed = urlparse(url)
    if parsed.scheme not in ("http", "https"):
        raise ValueError("URL must use HTTP or HTTPS.")

    host = parsed.hostname
    if not host:
        raise ValueError("URL must include a host name.")

    forbidden_hosts = {"localhost", "127.0.0.1", "0.0.0.0", "169.254.169.254", "::1"}
    if host in forbidden_hosts or host.endswith((".local", ".localhost", ".internal")):
        raise ValueError("Configuring internal or private network URLs is strictly prohibited.")

    try:
        ip = ipaddress.ip_address(host)
    except ValueError:
        ip = None  # a normal domain name
    if ip and (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved or ip.is_multicast):
        raise ValueError("Configuring internal or private network URLs is strictly prohibited.")


class SourceUpdate(BaseModel):
    is_active: bool
    rate_limit_rps: float = Field(gt=0, le=10)
    base_url: str


@router.get("/kpis")
def get_dashboard_kpis(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Calculates real-time, database-backed platform KPIs."""
    seven_days_ago = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(days=7)

    return {
        "total_active_offers": db.query(InternshipOffer).count(),
        "recent_offers_7d": db.query(InternshipOffer)
        .filter(InternshipOffer.first_seen_at >= seven_days_ago)
        .count(),
        "active_subscribers": db.query(User)
        .filter(User.is_active == True, User.role.in_(["USER", "SUBSCRIBER"]))  # noqa: E712
        .count(),
        "active_sources": db.query(ScrapingSource).filter(ScrapingSource.is_active == True).count(),  # noqa: E712
        "pending_notifications": db.query(NotificationLog).filter(NotificationLog.status == "QUEUED").count(),
        "failed_notifications": db.query(NotificationLog).filter(NotificationLog.status == "FAILED").count(),
    }


@router.get("/analytics")
def get_platform_analytics(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Builds chart payloads from exact database aggregations."""
    countries = (
        db.query(InternshipOffer.country, func.count(InternshipOffer.id))
        .group_by(InternshipOffer.country)
        .all()
    )
    notifications = (
        db.query(NotificationLog.status, func.count(NotificationLog.id))
        .group_by(NotificationLog.status)
        .all()
    )
    types = (
        db.query(InternshipOffer.internship_type, func.count(InternshipOffer.id))
        .group_by(InternshipOffer.internship_type)
        .all()
    )

    return {
        "offers_by_country": [{"name": c[0], "value": c[1]} for c in countries if c[0]],
        "notification_rates": [{"name": n[0], "value": n[1]} for n in notifications if n[0]],
        "offers_by_type": [{"name": t[0], "value": t[1]} for t in types if t[0]],
    }


@router.get("/sources")
def list_managed_sources(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    sources = db.query(ScrapingSource).order_by(ScrapingSource.name).all()
    return [
        {
            "id": s.id,
            "name": s.name,
            "slug": s.slug,
            "base_url": s.base_url,
            "is_active": s.is_active,
            "rate_limit_rps": s.rate_limit_rps,
            "created_at": s.created_at,
        }
        for s in sources
    ]


@router.put("/sources/{source_id}")
def update_source_config(source_id: int, payload: SourceUpdate, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    source = db.get(ScrapingSource, source_id)
    if not source:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Source not found")

    try:
        validate_safe_url(payload.base_url)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))

    source.is_active = payload.is_active
    source.rate_limit_rps = payload.rate_limit_rps
    source.base_url = payload.base_url
    db.commit()
    return {"message": "Source updated successfully"}


@router.post("/sources/{source_id}/trigger")
def trigger_manual_scrape(source_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Allows admins to inject an out-of-band scraping task immediately."""
    if not db.get(ScrapingSource, source_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Source not found")
    task = scrape_source_task.delay(source_id)
    return {"message": "Scrape queued", "task_id": str(task.id)}


@router.get("/runs")
def list_scraping_runs(limit: int = 50, offset: int = 0, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    limit = max(1, min(limit, 200))
    runs = (
        db.query(ScrapingRun)
        .options(joinedload(ScrapingRun.source))
        .order_by(ScrapingRun.started_at.desc())
        .offset(max(0, offset))
        .limit(limit)
        .all()
    )
    return [
        {
            "id": run.id,
            "source_name": run.source.name,
            "status": run.status,
            "items_found": run.items_found,
            "items_new": run.items_new,
            "started_at": run.started_at,
            "finished_at": run.finished_at,
            "duration_seconds": (run.finished_at - run.started_at).total_seconds()
            if run.finished_at and run.started_at
            else None,
            "error_log": run.error_log,
        }
        for run in runs
    ]
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/api/v1/analytics.py

```python
from collections import Counter
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer

router = APIRouter()

@router.get("/overview")
def get_analytics_overview(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    total = db.query(func.count(InternshipOffer.id)).scalar() or 0
    countries = (db.query(InternshipOffer.country, func.count(InternshipOffer.id))
                 .filter(InternshipOffer.country.isnot(None))
                 .group_by(InternshipOffer.country)
                 .order_by(func.count(InternshipOffer.id).desc()).limit(5).all())
    skills = Counter()
    for (s,) in db.query(InternshipOffer.required_skills).limit(5000):
        skills.update(s or [])
    return {"total_offers": total,
            "top_skills": [k for k, _ in skills.most_common(5)],
            "top_countries": [c for c, _ in countries]}
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/api/v1/auth.py

```python
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordRequestForm
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr, Field
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.api.deps import get_current_user
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.core.security import create_access_token, get_password_hash, verify_password
from app.models.preference import Preference
from app.models.user import User, UserRole

router = APIRouter()


class UserRegister(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str = Field(min_length=8, max_length=128)


class TokenResponse(BaseModel):
    access_token: str
    token_type: str = "bearer"


@router.post("/register", response_model=TokenResponse)
def register(payload: UserRegister, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    email = payload.email.lower()
    if db.query(User).filter(User.email == email).first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Email is already registered")

    user = User(
        email=email,
        hashed_password=get_password_hash(payload.password),
        role=UserRole.USER.value,
    )
    db.add(user)
    db.flush()  # assigns user.id
    db.add(Preference(user_id=user.id))  # default preference profile
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)

    return {"access_token": create_access_token(user.id), "token_type": "bearer"}


@router.post("/login", response_model=TokenResponse)
def login(form_data: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == form_data.username.lower()).first()
    if not user or not verify_password(form_data.password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid email or password")
    if not user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Inactive user")

    return {"access_token": create_access_token(user.id), "token_type": "bearer"}


@router.get("/me")
def me(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return {"id": current_user.id, "email": current_user.email, "role": current_user.role}
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/api/v1/matches.py

```python
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.api.deps import get_current_user
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.models.match import OfferMatch
from app.models.user import User

router = APIRouter()

@router.get("")
def my_matches(limit: int = 50, db: Session = Depends(get_db), user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    rows = (db.query(OfferMatch).filter(OfferMatch.user_id == user.id)
            .order_by(OfferMatch.score.desc()).limit(min(limit, 200)).all())
    return [{"id": m.id, "score": m.score, "reasons": m.reasons,
             "offer": {"id": m.offer.id, "title": m.offer.title, "company": m.offer.company,
                       "country": m.offer.country, "canonical_url": m.offer.canonical_url,
                       "internship_type": m.offer.internship_type}} for m in rows]
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/api/v1/offers.py

```python
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.db import get_db
from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("")
def list_offers(
    country: Optional[str] = None,
    search: Optional[str] = None,
    limit: int = 20,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    query = db.query(InternshipOffer)
    if country:
        query = query.filter(InternshipOffer.country.ilike(f"%{country}%"))
    if search:
        query = query.filter(InternshipOffer.title.ilike(f"%{search}%"))
    return query.order_by(InternshipOffer.first_seen_at.desc()).limit(max(1, min(limit, 100))).all()


@router.get("/filters")
def get_filter_options(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    countries = db.query(InternshipOffer.country).distinct().all()
    return {
        "countries": [c[0] for c in countries if c[0]],
        "popular_skills": ["python", "pytorch", "sql", "tensorflow", "docker", "spark"],
    }
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/api/v1/preferences.py

```python
import logging
from typing import List, Literal, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.api.deps import get_current_user
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.models.preference import Preference
from app.models.user import User

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
router = APIRouter()
LIST_FIELDS = ["target_roles", "preferred_countries", "preferred_cities", "workplace_types",
               "internship_types", "skills", "preferred_languages", "keywords", "exclusion_keywords"]


class PreferenceUpdate(BaseModel):
    target_roles: Optional[List[str]] = None
    preferred_countries: Optional[List[str]] = None
    preferred_cities: Optional[List[str]] = None
    workplace_types: Optional[List[str]] = None
    internship_types: Optional[List[str]] = None
    skills: Optional[List[str]] = None
    preferred_languages: Optional[List[str]] = None
    keywords: Optional[List[str]] = None
    exclusion_keywords: Optional[List[str]] = None
    alert_frequency: Optional[Literal["INSTANT", "DAILY", "WEEKLY", "OFF"]] = None
    min_relevance_score: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100)


def _get_or_create(db: Session, user: User) -> Preference:
    pref = db.query(Preference).filter_by(user_id=user.id).first()
    if not pref:
        pref = Preference(user_id=user.id)
        db.add(pref); db.commit(); db.refresh(pref)
    return pref


def _serialize(p: Preference) -> dict:
    d = {f: getattr(p, f) or [] for f in LIST_FIELDS}
    d.update(alert_frequency=p.alert_frequency, min_relevance_score=p.min_relevance_score)
    return d


@router.get("")
def get_preferences(db: Session = Depends(get_db), user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return _serialize(_get_or_create(db, user))


@router.put("")
def update_preferences(payload: PreferenceUpdate, db: Session = Depends(get_db),
                       user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    pref = _get_or_create(db, user)
    for key, value in payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
        if value is not None:
            setattr(pref, key, value)
    db.commit()
    try:
        from app.tasks.matcher import rematch_subscriber_preferences_task
        rematch_subscriber_preferences_task.delay(user.id)
    except Exception as exc:  # broker down must not fail the save
        logger.warning("Could not queue rematch: %s", exc)
    return _serialize(pref)
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/api/v1/subscriptions.py

```python
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.models.preference import AlertFrequency
from app.models.user import User

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/unsubscribe")
def unsubscribe(token: str = Query(...), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Validates the signed token and turns off email alerts for the user."""
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithms=["HS256"])
        if payload.get("action") != "unsubscribe":
            raise ValueError("wrong action")
        user_id = int(payload["user_id"])
    except (JWTError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired unsubscribe token.")

    user = db.get(User, user_id)
    if not user or not user.preference:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Subscriber not found.")

    user.preference.alert_frequency = AlertFrequency.OFF.value
    db.commit()
    return {"message": "You have been successfully unsubscribed from all email alerts."}
```

    broker=settings.REDIS_URL,
    backend=settings.REDIS_URL,
    include=["app.tasks.scraping_tasks", "app.tasks.matcher", "app.tasks.notifications"],

    task_serializer="json", accept_content=["json"], result_serializer="json",

    task_time_limit=3600, task_soft_time_limit=3300,
)

    "dispatch-enabled-scrapers-every-6-hours": {
        "task": "app.tasks.scraping_tasks.dispatch_scraping_tasks",
        "schedule": crontab(minute=0, hour="*/6"),
    },
    "process-daily-email-digests": {
        "task": "app.tasks.notifications.process_daily_digests",
        "schedule": crontab(minute=0, hour=8),
    },
}

```python
from typing import Optional
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=".env", case_sensitive=True, extra="ignore")

    PROJECT_NAME: str = "InternshipRadar AI"
    API_V1_STR: str = "/api/v1"
    ENVIRONMENT: str = "development"

    POSTGRES_SERVER: str = "postgres"
    POSTGRES_USER: str = "postgres"
    POSTGRES_PASSWORD: str = "postgres"
    POSTGRES_DB: str = "internship_radar"
    DATABASE_URL: Optional[str] = None
    REDIS_URL: str = "redis://redis:6379/0"

    SECRET_KEY: str = "change-me"
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = 1440
    CORS_ORIGINS: str = "http://localhost:3000"
    PUBLIC_API_URL: str = "http://localhost:8000/api/v1"

    EMAIL_PROVIDER: str = "console"
    EMAIL_FROM: str = "alerts@internshipradar.ai"
    SMTP_HOST: str = ""
    SMTP_PORT: int = 587
    SMTP_USER: str = ""
    SMTP_PASSWORD: str = ""
    RESEND_API_KEY: str = ""

    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(**kwargs)
        if not self.DATABASE_URL:
            self.DATABASE_URL = (
                f"postgresql+psycopg2://{self.POSTGRES_USER}:{self.POSTGRES_PASSWORD}"
                f"@{self.POSTGRES_SERVER}:5432/{self.POSTGRES_DB}"
            )
        if self.ENVIRONMENT == "production" and self.SECRET_KEY == "change-me":
            raise RuntimeError("Set a real SECRET_KEY in production.")


settings = Settings()
```

    settings.DATABASE_URL,
    pool_pre_ping=True,
)

Base = declarative_base()


def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()

## internship-platform/backend/app/core/locks.py

```python
import contextlib
import logging
import redis
from app.core.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Initialize a synchronous Redis client for lock management
redis_client = redis.Redis.from_url(
    getattr(settings, "REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379/0"),
    decode_responses=True
)

@contextlib.contextmanager
def redis_lock(lock_name: str, timeout: int = 1800):
    """
    Acquires a non-blocking distributed lock using Redis SETNX.
    Yields True if acquired, False otherwise.
    Auto-expires after `timeout` seconds to prevent deadlocks.
    """
    lock_key = f"lock:{lock_name}"
    acquired = redis_client.set(lock_key, "locked", nx=True, ex=timeout)
    
    try:
        yield acquired
    finally:
        if acquired:
            try:
                redis_client.delete(lock_key)
            except redis.RedisError as e:
                logger.error(f"Failed to release lock {lock_key}: {e}")
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/core/security.py

```python
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Optional, Union

from jose import jwt
from passlib.context import CryptContext

from app.core.config import settings

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")

ALGORITHM = "HS256"
SECRET_KEY = settings.SECRET_KEY


def _truncate_to_72_bytes(password: str) -> str:
    # bcrypt only uses the first 72 bytes of a password. Cut on a byte
    # boundary and drop any partial multi-byte character left at the end.
    return password.encode("utf-8")[:72].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")


def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    return pwd_context.verify(_truncate_to_72_bytes(plain_password), hashed_password)


def get_password_hash(password: str) -> str:
    return pwd_context.hash(_truncate_to_72_bytes(password))


def create_access_token(
    subject: Union[str, Any], expires_delta: Optional[timedelta] = None
) -> str:
    if expires_delta is None:
        expires_delta = timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
    expire = datetime.utcnow() + expires_delta
    to_encode = {"exp": expire, "sub": str(subject)}
    return jwt.encode(to_encode, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/db/seed.py

```python
"""
Synthetic Development Seed Script.
Populates PostgreSQL with an initial admin account, a source, and one synthetic offer.

Run `alembic upgrade head` first: this script does NOT create tables.
"""
from datetime import datetime

import app.models  # noqa: F401  (registers all ORM models)
from app.core.db import SessionLocal
from app.core.security import get_password_hash
from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer
from app.models.scraping import ScrapingSource
from app.models.user import User, UserRole


def seed_database():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        # 1. Admin user (development credentials: change them outside local dev)
        admin = db.query(User).filter(User.email == "admin@internshipradar.ai").first()
        if not admin:
            admin = User(
                email="admin@internshipradar.ai",
                hashed_password=get_password_hash("Admin123456!"),
                role=UserRole.ADMIN.value,
                is_active=True,
            )
            db.add(admin)
            db.commit()

        # 2. Default scraping source
        source = db.query(ScrapingSource).filter(
            ScrapingSource.slug == "abg_master_internships").first()
        if not source:
            source = ScrapingSource(
                name="ABG - Stages Master 2 / Ingénieur",
                slug="abg_master_internships",
                base_url="https://www.abg.asso.fr",
                feed_url=None,
                is_active=False,  # enable once you've confirmed ABG's terms allow it
                rate_limit_rps=0.5,
            )
            db.add(source)
            db.commit()

        # 3. Synthetic internship listing
        offer = (
            db.query(InternshipOffer)
            .filter(InternshipOffer.canonical_url == "https://synth.example.com/pfe-ml-1")
            .first()
        )
        if not offer:
            now = datetime.utcnow()
            offer = InternshipOffer(
                source_id=source.id,
                source_item_id="SYNTH-001",
                canonical_url="https://synth.example.com/pfe-ml-1",
                fingerprint="synth_pfe_ml_fingerprint_001",
                title="Synthetic PFE - Applied AI / Computer Vision Research Intern",
                normalized_title="synthetic pfe - applied ai / computer vision research intern",
                company="Synthetic AI Research Labs",
                location_raw="Paris, France",
                country="France",
                workplace_type="hybrid",
                internship_type="pfe",
                description="Synthetic end-of-studies internship position focusing on PyTorch, Object Detection, and LLMs.",
                required_skills=["python", "pytorch", "opencv", "docker"],
                languages=["English", "French"],
                published_at=now,
                first_seen_at=now,
                last_seen_at=now,
            )
            db.add(offer)
            db.commit()

        print("Synthetic seed completed successfully.")
    finally:
        db.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    seed_database()
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/models/match.py

```python
"""
Offer Match Model.
Stores structured match records between Subscribers and Internship Offers.
"""
from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import Column, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Integer, JSON, String, UniqueConstraint
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship

from app.core.db import Base


class OfferMatch(Base):
    __tablename__ = "offer_matches"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True)
    offer_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("internship_offers.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True)

    score = Column(Float, nullable=False, index=True)
    breakdown = Column(JSON, nullable=False, default=dict)
    reasons = Column(JSON, nullable=False, default=list)
    algorithm_version = Column(String(50), nullable=False, default="1.0.0-deterministic")

    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="matches")
    offer = relationship("InternshipOffer", backref="matches")
    notifications = relationship(
        "NotificationLog", back_populates="match", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )

    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("user_id", "offer_id", name="uq_user_offer_match"),
    )
```

class NotificationLog(Base):

    match_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("offer_matches.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, unique=True)
    channel = Column(String(50), default="email")
    status = Column(String(50), default="QUEUED", index=True)  # QUEUED / SENT / FAILED

    sent_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)

## internship-platform/backend/app/models/offer.py

```python
"""
Internship Offer Model.
"""
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, Text, DateTime, ForeignKey, JSON
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.core.db import Base


class InternshipOffer(Base):
    __tablename__ = "internship_offers"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    source_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("scraping_sources.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    source_item_id = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    canonical_url = Column(String(1024), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    fingerprint = Column(String(64), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)

    title = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    normalized_title = Column(String(255), index=True)
    company = Column(String(255), index=True, nullable=False)
    location_raw = Column(String(255))
    country = Column(String(100), index=True)
    workplace_type = Column(String(50))  # remote, hybrid, onsite
    internship_type = Column(String(50)) # pfe, standard
    description = Column(Text)
    required_skills = Column(JSON, default=list)
    languages = Column(JSON, default=list)

    published_at = Column(DateTime)
    application_deadline = Column(DateTime)
    first_seen_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    last_seen_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)

    source = relationship("ScrapingSource", back_populates="offers")
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/models/preference.py

```python
"""
Subscriber Preference Model.
Stores configurable filter criteria for matching internship offers.
"""
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum

from sqlalchemy import Column, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Integer, JSON, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship

from app.core.db import Base


class AlertFrequency(str, Enum):
    INSTANT = "INSTANT"
    DAILY = "DAILY"
    WEEKLY = "WEEKLY"
    OFF = "OFF"


class Preference(Base):
    __tablename__ = "preferences"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), unique=True, nullable=False)

    preferred_countries = Column(JSON, default=list)
    preferred_cities = Column(JSON, default=list)
    workplace_types = Column(JSON, default=list)   # remote, hybrid, onsite
    internship_types = Column(JSON, default=list)  # pfe, standard
    target_roles = Column(JSON, default=list)
    skills = Column(JSON, default=list)
    preferred_languages = Column(JSON, default=list)
    keywords = Column(JSON, default=list)
    exclusion_keywords = Column(JSON, default=list)

    min_relevance_score = Column(Float, default=50.0)
    alert_frequency = Column(String(20), default=AlertFrequency.DAILY.value)

    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="preference")
```

"""
Scraping models: sources and run history.

from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Column, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text

from app.core.db import Base


class RunStatus(str, Enum):
    PENDING = "PENDING"
    SUCCESS = "SUCCESS"
    FAILED = "FAILED"


class ScrapingSource(Base):
    __tablename__ = "scraping_sources"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    slug = Column(String(100), unique=True, nullable=False, index=True)
    base_url = Column(String(512), nullable=False)
    feed_url = Column(String(512), nullable=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    rate_limit_rps = Column(Float, default=1.0)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

    offers = relationship("InternshipOffer", back_populates="source", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    runs = relationship("ScrapingRun", back_populates="source", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

    source = relationship("ScrapingSource", back_populates="runs")

    ADMIN = "ADMIN"
    SUBSCRIBER = "SUBSCRIBER"

    __tablename__ = "users"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    email = Column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False, index=True)

## internship-platform/backend/app/services/email/base.py

```python
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import List, Optional

class BaseEmailProvider(ABC):
    """Abstract interface for all email delivery providers."""
    
    @abstractmethod
    def send_email(
        self, 
        to_email: str, 
        subject: str, 
        html_content: str, 
        text_content: str
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Dispatches an email and returns True if accepted by the provider.
        Raises an exception if the provider returns a transient error.
        """
        pass
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/services/email/console.py

```python
import logging
from app.services.email.base import BaseEmailProvider

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class ConsoleEmailProvider(BaseEmailProvider):
    """Development fallback provider that prints emails to the console."""
    
    def send_email(self, to_email: str, subject: str, html_content: str, text_content: str) -> bool:
        logger.info(f"========== EMAIL DISPATCHED TO: {to_email} ==========")
        logger.info(f"SUBJECT: {subject}")
        logger.info(f"TEXT BODY:\n{text_content}")
        logger.info("=======================================================")
        return True
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/services/email/factory.py

```python
from app.services.email.base import BaseEmailProvider
from app.services.email.console import ConsoleEmailProvider
from app.services.email.smtp import SMTPEmailProvider
from app.services.email.resend_adapter import ResendEmailProvider
from app.core.config import settings

def get_email_provider() -> BaseEmailProvider:
    """Returns the configured email provider, falling back to Console for dev."""
    provider_name = getattr(settings, "EMAIL_PROVIDER", "console").lower()
    
    if provider_name == "resend" and getattr(settings, "RESEND_API_KEY", ""):
        return ResendEmailProvider()
    elif provider_name == "smtp" and getattr(settings, "SMTP_HOST", ""):
        return SMTPEmailProvider()
    
    return ConsoleEmailProvider()
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/services/email/resend_adapter.py

```python
import resend
import logging
from app.services.email.base import BaseEmailProvider
from app.core.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class ResendEmailProvider(BaseEmailProvider):
    """Resend API adapter for email delivery."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        resend.api_key = getattr(settings, "RESEND_API_KEY", "")
        self.sender = getattr(settings, "EMAIL_FROM", "alerts@internshipradar.ai")

    def send_email(self, to_email: str, subject: str, html_content: str, text_content: str) -> bool:
        if "\n" in to_email or "\r" in to_email:
            raise ValueError("Invalid email address format.")
            
        try:
            response = resend.Emails.send({
                "from": self.sender,
                "to": to_email,
                "subject": subject,
                "html": html_content,
                "text": text_content
            })
            logger.info(f"Resend delivered email to {to_email}, ID: {response.get('id')}")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Resend Delivery failed for {to_email}: {e}")
            raise
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/services/email/smtp.py

```python
import smtplib
import logging
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart
from app.services.email.base import BaseEmailProvider
from app.core.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class SMTPEmailProvider(BaseEmailProvider):
    """Standard SMTP adapter for email delivery."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.host = getattr(settings, "SMTP_HOST", "")
        self.port = getattr(settings, "SMTP_PORT", 587)
        self.user = getattr(settings, "SMTP_USER", "")
        self.password = getattr(settings, "SMTP_PASSWORD", "")
        self.sender = getattr(settings, "EMAIL_FROM", "alerts@internshipradar.ai")

    def send_email(self, to_email: str, subject: str, html_content: str, text_content: str) -> bool:
        msg = MIMEMultipart("alternative")
        msg["Subject"] = subject
        msg["From"] = self.sender
        msg["To"] = to_email

        # Prevent Header Injection
        if "\n" in to_email or "\r" in to_email:
            raise ValueError("Invalid email address format.")

        msg.attach(MIMEText(text_content, "plain"))
        msg.attach(MIMEText(html_content, "html"))

        try:
            with smtplib.SMTP(self.host, self.port) as server:
                server.starttls()
                server.login(self.user, self.password)
                server.sendmail(self.sender, to_email, msg.as_string())
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"SMTP Delivery failed for {to_email}: {e}")
            raise
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/services/email/templates.py

```python
import html

from jose import jwt

from app.core.config import settings


def _e(value) -> str:
    """HTML-escape any scraped value (None becomes an empty string)."""
    return html.escape("" if value is None else str(value), quote=True)


def _one_line(value) -> str:
    """Collapse whitespace/newlines so values are safe inside an email subject."""
    return " ".join(str(value or "").split())


def generate_unsubscribe_token(user_id) -> str:
    """Generates a signed, stateless token for single-click unsubscribe."""
    return jwt.encode(
        {"user_id": str(user_id), "action": "unsubscribe"},
        settings.SECRET_KEY,
        algorithm="HS256",
    )


def generate_match_email(user, matches: list) -> dict:
    """
    Generates HTML and plain text payloads for a list of internship matches.
    Handles both single instant matches and daily digests.
    """
    unsub_token = generate_unsubscribe_token(user.id)
    unsub_url = f"{settings.PUBLIC_API_URL}/subscriptions/unsubscribe?token={unsub_token}"

    is_digest = len(matches) > 1
    if is_digest:
        subject = f"Your Daily Internship Digest - {len(matches)} New Matches"
    else:
        subject = f"New Internship Match: {_one_line(matches[0].offer.title)}"

    # ---- Plain text ----
    text_lines = [f"Hello, we found {len(matches)} new internship(s) matching your criteria:\n"]
    for match in matches:
        offer = match.offer
        text_lines.append(f"- {offer.title} at {offer.company} ({offer.country or 'n/a'})")
        text_lines.append(f"  Match Score: {match.score}% | Type: {offer.internship_type or 'n/a'}")
        text_lines.append(f"  Apply here: {offer.canonical_url}\n")
    text_lines.append(f"\nUnsubscribe or manage your alerts here: {unsub_url}")
    text_content = "\n".join(text_lines)

    # ---- HTML ----
    html_lines = [
        "<html><body style='font-family: Arial, sans-serif; color: #333; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto;'>",
        "<h2 style='color: #2563eb;'>InternshipRadar AI</h2>",
        f"<p>Hello, we found <strong>{len(matches)} new opportunity(s)</strong> matching your profile.</p>",
    ]

    for match in matches:
        offer = match.offer
        published = offer.published_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") if offer.published_at else "Recent"
        first_reason = match.reasons[0] if match.reasons else "General match"
        workplace = (offer.workplace_type or "n/a").capitalize()
        internship_type = (offer.internship_type or "n/a").upper()
        html_lines.append(
            "<div style='border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; padding: 15px; margin-bottom: 15px; border-radius: 8px;'>"
            f"<h3 style='margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 5px; color: #1f2937;'>{_e(offer.title)}</h3>"
            f"<p style='margin: 0 0 10px 0; color: #4b5563;'><strong>{_e(offer.company)}</strong>"
            f" • {_e(offer.location_raw)} • {_e(workplace)}</p>"
            f"<p style='font-size: 14px;'><strong>Score:</strong> {match.score}% • "
            f"<strong>Type:</strong> {_e(internship_type)} • <strong>Published:</strong> {_e(published)}</p>"
            f"<p style='font-size: 13px; color: #6b7280; font-style: italic;'>Why it matched: {_e(first_reason)}</p>"
            f"<a href='{_e(offer.canonical_url)}' style='display: inline-block; background: #2563eb; "
            "color: #fff; padding: 8px 16px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 4px; "
            "font-weight: bold;'>View Application</a>"
            "</div>"
        )

    html_lines.append(
        "<div style='margin-top: 30px; font-size: 12px; color: #9ca3af;'>"
        "<p>You are receiving this because you subscribed to InternshipRadar alerts.</p>"
        f"<p><a href='{_e(unsub_url)}' style='color: #9ca3af; text-decoration: underline;'>"
        "Unsubscribe or change your alert frequency</a></p>"
        "</div></body></html>"
    )

    return {
        "subject": subject,
        "text_content": text_content,
        "html_content": "".join(html_lines),
    }
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/services/ingestion.py

```python
import hashlib
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple
from dateutil import parser as date_parser
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer
from app.models.scraping import ScrapingSource

CONTENT_FIELDS = ("title", "description", "company")
UPDATABLE = ("title", "company", "location_raw", "country", "workplace_type",
             "internship_type", "description", "required_skills", "languages")


def _fingerprint(raw: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    sig = f"{raw.get('company','')}|{raw.get('title','')}|{raw.get('country','')}".lower()
    return hashlib.sha256(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9|]", "", sig).encode()).hexdigest()


def _parse_dt(value: Any) -> Optional[datetime]:
    if not value:
        return None
    dt = value if isinstance(value, datetime) else date_parser.parse(str(value))
    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None) if dt.tzinfo else dt


def process_and_upsert_offer(
    db: Session, source: ScrapingSource, raw_item: Dict[str, Any]
) -> Tuple[InternshipOffer, bool, bool]:
    now = datetime.utcnow()
    canonical_url = str(raw_item.get("canonical_url"))
    fingerprint = raw_item.get("fingerprint") or _fingerprint(raw_item)

    existing = (
        db.query(InternshipOffer)
        .filter((InternshipOffer.canonical_url == canonical_url)
                | (InternshipOffer.fingerprint == fingerprint))
        .first()
    )

    if existing:
        # Only compare fields the scraper actually supplied.
        content_changed = any(
            k in raw_item and getattr(existing, k) != raw_item[k] for k in CONTENT_FIELDS
        )
        for field in UPDATABLE:
            if raw_item.get(field) is not None:
                setattr(existing, field, raw_item[field])
        if raw_item.get("title"):
            existing.normalized_title = raw_item.get("normalized_title") or raw_item["title"].lower()
        existing.last_seen_at = now
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(existing)
        return existing, False, content_changed

    title = raw_item.get("title") or ""
    new_offer = InternshipOffer(
        source_id=source.id,
        source_item_id=raw_item.get("source_item_id"),
        canonical_url=canonical_url,
        fingerprint=fingerprint,
        title=title,
        normalized_title=raw_item.get("normalized_title") or title.lower(),
        company=raw_item.get("company"),
        location_raw=raw_item.get("location_raw", "Europe"),
        country=raw_item.get("country", "Europe"),
        workplace_type=raw_item.get("workplace_type", "unknown"),
        internship_type=raw_item.get("internship_type", "pfe"),
        description=raw_item.get("description", ""),
        required_skills=raw_item.get("required_skills", []),
        languages=raw_item.get("languages", []),
        published_at=_parse_dt(raw_item.get("published_at")),
        first_seen_at=now,
        last_seen_at=now,
    )
    db.add(new_offer)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(new_offer)
    return new_offer, True, False
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/services/matcher.py

```python
"""
Internship Relevance and Ranking Engine.
Computes deterministic, explainable match scores between Internship Offers and Subscriber Preferences.
Supports configurable feature weights, hard exclusions, and optional embedding interfaces.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional
import re

ALGORITHM_VERSION = "1.0.0-deterministic"

# Feature Weights (Sum = 100)
WEIGHTS = {
    "role": 30.0,
    "skills": 25.0,
    "location": 20.0,
    "internship_type": 10.0,
    "language": 10.0,
    "keywords": 5.0,
}


def normalize_text(text: Optional[str]) -> str:
    """Safely normalizes text to lowercase stripped string."""
    if not text:
        return ""
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", str(text).lower().strip())


def normalize_list(items: Optional[List[Any]]) -> List[str]:
    """Safely normalizes a list of strings or JSON-serialized arrays."""
    if not items:
        return []
    res = []
    for item in items:
        if isinstance(item, str):
            res.append(normalize_text(item))
        elif isinstance(item, list):
            res.extend([normalize_text(i) for i in item if i])
    return [i for i in res if i]


def calculate_match_score(offer: Any, preference: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Calculates a deterministic relevance score between 0.0 and 100.0.
    Returns score, breakdown, match features, hard exclusion flags, and algorithm version.
    """
    reasons = []
    breakdown = {k: 0.0 for k in WEIGHTS.keys()}
    
    # Extract normalized offer attributes
    offer_title = normalize_text(getattr(offer, "title", ""))
    offer_norm_title = normalize_text(getattr(offer, "normalized_title", ""))
    offer_company = normalize_text(getattr(offer, "company", ""))
    offer_desc = normalize_text(getattr(offer, "description", ""))
    offer_combined_text = f"{offer_title} {offer_norm_title} {offer_company} {offer_desc}"
    
    offer_skills = set(normalize_list(getattr(offer, "required_skills", [])))
    offer_languages = set(normalize_list(getattr(offer, "languages", [])))
    offer_country = normalize_text(getattr(offer, "country", ""))
    offer_location = normalize_text(getattr(offer, "location_raw", ""))
    offer_workplace = normalize_text(getattr(offer, "workplace_type", ""))  # remote, hybrid, onsite
    offer_type = normalize_text(getattr(offer, "internship_type", ""))      # pfe, standard, etc.

    # Extract normalized user preferences
    target_roles = normalize_list(getattr(preference, "target_roles", []))
    pref_skills = set(normalize_list(getattr(preference, "skills", [])))
    pref_countries = set(normalize_list(getattr(preference, "preferred_countries", [])))
    pref_workplaces = set(normalize_list(getattr(preference, "workplace_types", [])))
    pref_types = set(normalize_list(getattr(preference, "internship_types", [])))
    pref_languages = set(normalize_list(getattr(preference, "preferred_languages", [])))
    keywords = normalize_list(getattr(preference, "keywords", []))
    exclusion_keywords = normalize_list(getattr(preference, "exclusion_keywords", []))

    # --- 1. HARD EXCLUSION CHECKS ---
    for ex_kw in exclusion_keywords:
        if ex_kw and ex_kw in offer_combined_text:
            reasons.append(f"Excluded by negative keyword: '{ex_kw}'")
            return {
                "score": 0.0,
                "is_excluded": True,
                "exclusion_reason": f"Excluded by negative keyword: '{ex_kw}'",
                "breakdown": breakdown,
                "reasons": reasons,
                "algorithm_version": ALGORITHM_VERSION,
            }

    # Hard Exclusion for PFE-only preference
    if pref_types and "pfe" in pref_types and offer_type and offer_type != "pfe":
        # If user explicitly specified PFE and offer is explicitly non-PFE
        reasons.append(f"Excluded by internship type mismatch: Required PFE, got '{offer_type}'")
        return {
            "score": 0.0,
            "is_excluded": True,
            "exclusion_reason": f"Required PFE internship type, got '{offer_type}'",
            "breakdown": breakdown,
            "reasons": reasons,
            "algorithm_version": ALGORITHM_VERSION,
        }

    # --- 2. ROLE RELEVANCE (Weight: 30) ---
    role_score = 0.0
    if target_roles:
        matched_roles = [r for r in target_roles if r in offer_title or r in offer_norm_title]
        if matched_roles:
            role_score = WEIGHTS["role"]
            reasons.append(f"Role match: found {matched_roles}")
        else:
            reasons.append("No explicit target role match found in job title")
    else:
        role_score = WEIGHTS["role"] * 0.5  # Neutral default if no preference specified
        reasons.append("No specific role preference specified; neutral score given")
    breakdown["role"] = role_score

    # --- 3. SKILL OVERLAP (Weight: 25) ---
    skill_score = 0.0
    if pref_skills:
        if offer_skills:
            intersection = pref_skills.intersection(offer_skills)
            overlap_ratio = len(intersection) / len(pref_skills)
            skill_score = WEIGHTS["skills"] * overlap_ratio
            reasons.append(f"Skill overlap: {len(intersection)}/{len(pref_skills)} skills matched ({list(intersection)})")
        else:
            # Check for skills in job description as partial fallback
            matched_in_desc = [s for s in pref_skills if s in offer_desc]
            if matched_in_desc:
                overlap_ratio = (len(matched_in_desc) / len(pref_skills)) * 0.7  # Partial credit
                skill_score = WEIGHTS["skills"] * overlap_ratio
                reasons.append(f"Partial skill match in text: {matched_in_desc}")
            else:
                reasons.append("No skill overlap found")
    else:
        skill_score = WEIGHTS["skills"] * 0.5
        reasons.append("No skill preferences specified; neutral score given")
    breakdown["skills"] = skill_score

    # --- 4. LOCATION & WORKPLACE COMPATIBILITY (Weight: 20) ---
    loc_score = 0.0
    loc_reasons = []
    
    # Country / Location check
    country_matched = False
    if pref_countries:
        if offer_country and offer_country in pref_countries:
            country_matched = True
            loc_reasons.append(f"Country match: {offer_country}")
        elif any(c in offer_location for c in pref_countries):
            country_matched = True
            loc_reasons.append("Country matched in raw location text")
    else:
        country_matched = True  # Any country allowed

    # Remote / Workplace type check
    workplace_matched = False
    if pref_workplaces:
        if offer_workplace and offer_workplace in pref_workplaces:
            workplace_matched = True
            loc_reasons.append(f"Workplace match: {offer_workplace}")
        elif "remote" in pref_workplaces and "remote" in offer_combined_text:
            workplace_matched = True
            loc_reasons.append("Remote match found in text")
    else:
        workplace_matched = True  # Any workplace allowed

    if country_matched and workplace_matched:
        loc_score = WEIGHTS["location"]
    elif country_matched or workplace_matched:
        loc_score = WEIGHTS["location"] * 0.5
        loc_reasons.append("Partial location/workplace match")
    else:
        loc_reasons.append("Location/workplace mismatch")

    reasons.append("; ".join(loc_reasons) if loc_reasons else "Location checked")
    breakdown["location"] = loc_score

    # --- 5. INTERNSHIP TYPE COMPATIBILITY (Weight: 10) ---
    type_score = 0.0
    if pref_types:
        if offer_type and offer_type in pref_types:
            type_score = WEIGHTS["internship_type"]
            reasons.append(f"Internship type match: {offer_type}")
        elif not offer_type:
            type_score = WEIGHTS["internship_type"] * 0.5
            reasons.append("Offer internship type unstated; partial neutral score")
    else:
        type_score = WEIGHTS["internship_type"]
    breakdown["internship_type"] = type_score

    # --- 6. LANGUAGE COMPATIBILITY (Weight: 10) ---
    lang_score = 0.0
    if pref_languages:
        if offer_languages:
            lang_intersection = pref_languages.intersection(offer_languages)
            if lang_intersection:
                lang_score = WEIGHTS["language"]
                reasons.append(f"Language match: {list(lang_intersection)}")
            else:
                reasons.append("Language mismatch")
        else:
            lang_score = WEIGHTS["language"] * 0.5
            reasons.append("Offer languages unspecified; partial neutral score")
    else:
        lang_score = WEIGHTS["language"]
    breakdown["language"] = lang_score

    # --- 7. POSITIVE KEYWORDS (Weight: 5) ---
    kw_score = 0.0
    if keywords:
        matched_kws = [kw for kw in keywords if kw in offer_combined_text]
        if matched_kws:
            kw_score = WEIGHTS["keywords"] * (len(matched_kws) / len(keywords))
            reasons.append(f"Keywords matched: {matched_kws}")
    else:
        kw_score = WEIGHTS["keywords"]
    breakdown["keywords"] = kw_score

    total_score = round(sum(breakdown.values()), 2)

    return {
        "score": total_score,
        "is_excluded": False,
        "exclusion_reason": None,
        "breakdown": breakdown,
        "reasons": reasons,
        "algorithm_version": ALGORITHM_VERSION,
    }


class OptionalSemanticMatcher:
    """Optional embedding-based semantic matcher fallback interface."""

    def __init__(self, enabled: bool = False):
        self.enabled = enabled

    def get_semantic_similarity(self, text_a: str, text_b: str) -> float:
        """Fallback interface returning 0.0 when external paid LLM APIs are not present."""
        if not self.enabled:
            return 0.0
        # Extensible for local HuggingFace sentences-transformers or embeddings
        return 0.0
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/tasks/matcher.py

```python
import logging
from celery import shared_task
from app.core.db import SessionLocal
from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer
from app.models.preference import Preference, AlertFrequency
from app.models.match import OfferMatch
from app.services.matcher import calculate_match_score

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _apply(db, offer, pref):
    """Create/update/remove the match for (user, offer). Returns (match, created)."""
    result = calculate_match_score(offer, pref)
    threshold = pref.min_relevance_score if pref.min_relevance_score is not None else 50.0
    qualifies = not result["is_excluded"] and result["score"] >= threshold
    existing = db.query(OfferMatch).filter_by(user_id=pref.user_id, offer_id=offer.id).first()

    if not qualifies:
        if existing and not existing.notifications:   # never delete already-notified matches
            db.delete(existing)
        return None, False
    if existing:
        existing.score = result["score"]
        existing.breakdown = result["breakdown"]
        existing.reasons = result["reasons"]
        existing.algorithm_version = result["algorithm_version"]
        return existing, False
    match = OfferMatch(user_id=pref.user_id, offer_id=offer.id, score=result["score"],
                       breakdown=result["breakdown"], reasons=result["reasons"],
                       algorithm_version=result["algorithm_version"])
    db.add(match)
    db.flush()
    return match, True


@shared_task(name="app.tasks.matcher.match_new_offer_task")
def match_new_offer_task(offer_id: int):
    from app.tasks.notifications import queue_match_notification_task
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        offer = db.get(InternshipOffer, offer_id)
        if not offer:
            return {"status": "error", "message": "Offer not found", "matches_created": 0}
        instant_ids, created = [], 0
        for pref in db.query(Preference).all():
            match, was_created = _apply(db, offer, pref)
            if was_created:
                created += 1
                if pref.alert_frequency == AlertFrequency.INSTANT.value:
                    instant_ids.append(match.id)
        db.commit()
        for match_id in instant_ids:          # queue only after commit
            queue_match_notification_task.delay(match_id)
        return {"offer_id": offer_id, "matches_created": created}
    finally:
        db.close()


@shared_task(name="app.tasks.matcher.rematch_subscriber_preferences_task")
def rematch_subscriber_preferences_task(user_id: int):
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        pref = db.query(Preference).filter_by(user_id=user_id).first()
        if not pref:
            return {"status": "error", "message": "No preferences"}
        for offer in db.query(InternshipOffer).all():
            _apply(db, offer, pref)
        db.commit()
        return {"status": "ok", "user_id": user_id}
    finally:
        db.close()
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/tasks/notifications.py

```python
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from celery import shared_task
from app.core.db import SessionLocal
from app.models.match import OfferMatch
from app.models.notification import NotificationLog
from app.models.preference import AlertFrequency, Preference
from app.models.user import User
from app.services.email.factory import get_email_provider
from app.services.email.templates import generate_match_email

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@shared_task(bind=True, name="app.tasks.notifications.queue_match_notification_task",
             max_retries=3, default_retry_delay=60)
def queue_match_notification_task(self, match_id: int):
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        match = db.get(OfferMatch, match_id)
        if not match or not match.user.is_active:
            return "Ignored: Match missing or user inactive."
        pref = match.user.preference
        if not pref or pref.alert_frequency != AlertFrequency.INSTANT.value:
            return "Ignored: User does not want instant alerts."

        log = db.query(NotificationLog).filter_by(match_id=match.id).first()
        if log and log.status != "FAILED":
            return f"Ignored: Notification already exists with status {log.status}"
        if log:
            log.status = "QUEUED"
        else:
            log = NotificationLog(user_id=match.user_id, match_id=match.id, status="QUEUED")
            db.add(log)
        db.commit()

        provider = get_email_provider()
        email = generate_match_email(match.user, [match])
        try:
            provider.send_email(to_email=match.user.email, subject=email["subject"],
                                html_content=email["html_content"], text_content=email["text_content"])
        except Exception as exc:
            log.status = "FAILED"
            db.commit()
            raise self.retry(exc=exc)

        log.status = "SENT"
        log.sent_at = datetime.utcnow()
        db.commit()
        return "Instant notification sent successfully."
    finally:
        db.close()


@shared_task(name="app.tasks.notifications.process_daily_digests")
def process_daily_digests():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        provider = get_email_provider()
        matches = (
            db.query(OfferMatch)
            .join(User, OfferMatch.user_id == User.id)
            .join(Preference, Preference.user_id == User.id)
            .filter(User.is_active.is_(True),
                    Preference.alert_frequency == AlertFrequency.DAILY.value,
                    ~OfferMatch.notifications.any())
            .all()
        )
        by_user = {}
        for m in matches:
            by_user.setdefault(m.user_id, []).append(m)

        dispatched = 0
        for user_id, user_matches in by_user.items():
            user = user_matches[0].user
            logs = [NotificationLog(user_id=user_id, match_id=m.id, status="QUEUED") for m in user_matches]
            db.add_all(logs)
            db.commit()
            email = generate_match_email(user, user_matches)
            try:
                provider.send_email(to_email=user.email, subject=email["subject"],
                                    html_content=email["html_content"], text_content=email["text_content"])
                status, sent_at = "SENT", datetime.utcnow()
                dispatched += 1
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error("Digest delivery failed for %s: %s", user.email, exc)
                status, sent_at = "FAILED", None
            for log in logs:
                log.status, log.sent_at = status, sent_at
            db.commit()
        return f"Processed digests for {len(by_user)} users. Sent: {dispatched}"
    finally:
        db.close()
```

## internship-platform/backend/app/tasks/scraping_tasks.py

```python
import json
import logging
import os
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime

from celery import shared_task

from app.core.db import SessionLocal
from app.core.locks import redis_lock
from app.models.scraping import RunStatus, ScrapingRun, ScrapingSource
from app.services.ingestion import process_and_upsert_offer
from app.tasks.matcher import match_new_offer_task

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@shared_task(name="app.tasks.scraping_tasks.dispatch_scraping_tasks")
def dispatch_scraping_tasks():
    """Dispatches individual asynchronous scraping tasks for all active sources."""
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        active_sources = db.query(ScrapingSource).filter(ScrapingSource.is_active == True).all()  # noqa: E712
        for source in active_sources:
            scrape_source_task.delay(source.id)
        return {"dispatched_sources": len(active_sources)}
    finally:
        db.close()


@shared_task(
    bind=True,
    name="app.tasks.scraping_tasks.scrape_source_task",
    max_retries=3,
    default_retry_delay=300,  # 5 minute backoff on failure
)
def scrape_source_task(self, source_id: int):
    """Executes a Scrapy spider as a subprocess and processes the scraped JSON output."""
    lock_name = f"scrape_lock_source_{source_id}"

    with redis_lock(lock_name, timeout=1800) as acquired:
        if not acquired:
            logger.warning(f"Source {source_id} is already being scraped. Skipping duplicate execution.")
            return {"status": "skipped_locked", "source_id": source_id}

        db = SessionLocal()
        run_record = None
        metrics = {"collected": 0, "new": 0, "updated": 0, "unchanged": 0, "skipped": 0}

        try:
            source = db.get(ScrapingSource, source_id)
            if not source:
                return {"status": "error", "error": "Source not found"}

            run_record = ScrapingRun(
                source_id=source.id,
                status=RunStatus.PENDING.value,
                started_at=datetime.utcnow(),
            )
            db.add(run_record)
            db.commit()

            # The spider name must equal source.slug.
            output_file = f"/tmp/offers_{run_record.id}.json"
            scraper_dir = "/app/scraper"
            command = ["scrapy", "crawl", source.slug, "-O", output_file]
            if source.rate_limit_rps and source.rate_limit_rps > 0:
                command += ["-s", f"DOWNLOAD_DELAY={1.0 / source.rate_limit_rps:.2f}"]

            result = subprocess.run(
                command, cwd=scraper_dir, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3000
            )
            if result.returncode != 0:
                raise Exception(f"Scrapy failed: {result.stderr[-2000:]}")

               if os.path.exists(output_file):
       raw_items = []
    if os.path.getsize(output_file) > 0:
           with open(output_file, "r") as f:
               raw_items = json.load(f)

       for raw_item in raw_items:
           metrics["collected"] += 1
           try:
               offer, is_new, is_updated = process_and_upsert_offer(db, source, raw_item)
           except Exception as item_exc:
               db.rollback()
               metrics["skipped"] += 1
               logger.warning("Skipping bad item %s: %s", raw_item.get("canonical_url"), item_exc)
               continue

           if is_new:
               metrics["new"] += 1
               match_new_offer_task.delay(offer.id)
           elif is_updated:
               metrics["updated"] += 1
           else:
               metrics["unchanged"] += 1

       os.remove(output_file)

            run_record.status = RunStatus.SUCCESS.value
            run_record.items_found = metrics["collected"]
            run_record.items_new = metrics["new"]
            run_record.items_updated = metrics["updated"]
            run_record.finished_at = datetime.utcnow()
            db.commit()

            return {"status": "success", "source_id": source_id, "metrics": metrics}

        except Exception as exc:
            db.rollback()
            if run_record is not None:
                run_record.status = RunStatus.FAILED.value
                run_record.error_log = str(exc)
                run_record.finished_at = datetime.utcnow()
                db.commit()
            logger.error(f"Scraping failed for source {source_id}: {exc}")
            raise self.retry(exc=exc)
        finally:
            db.close()
```
