## internship-platform/backend/tests/conftest.py

```python
import os
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import make_url

_base = make_url(os.environ.get(
    "DATABASE_URL", "postgresql+psycopg2://postgres:postgres@postgres:5432/internship_radar"))
_test_db = _base.database if _base.database.endswith("_test") else _base.database + "_test"
os.environ["DATABASE_URL"] = _base.set(database=_test_db).render_as_string(hide_password=False)
assert _test_db.endswith("_test")

_admin = create_engine(_base.set(database="postgres"), isolation_level="AUTOCOMMIT")
with _admin.connect() as c:
    if not c.execute(text("SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname=:n"), {"n": _test_db}).scalar():
        c.execute(text(f'CREATE DATABASE "{_test_db}"'))

from app.core.db import Base, SessionLocal, engine  # noqa: E402
from app.core.celery_app import celery_app          # noqa: E402
import app.models                                   # noqa: E402,F401

celery_app.conf.task_always_eager = True
celery_app.conf.task_eager_propagates = True


@pytest.fixture(scope="session", autouse=True)
def _schema():
    Base.metadata.drop_all(engine)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    yield


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_tables():
    names = ",".join(t.name for t in Base.metadata.sorted_tables)
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(text(f"TRUNCATE {names} RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE"))
    yield


@pytest.fixture
def db_session():
    s = SessionLocal()
    yield s
    s.close()
```

## internship-platform/backend/tests/test_admin.py

```python
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from app.main import app
from app.api.v1.admin import validate_safe_url

client = TestClient(app)

def test_admin_access_denied_for_unauthenticated():
    """Unauthenticated users should be rejected entirely."""
    response = client.get("/api/v1/admin/kpis")
    assert response.status_code == 401

def test_safe_url_validation_blocks_ssrf():
    """Tests the SSRF validation prevents internal network scans."""
    # Should Pass
    validate_safe_url("https://example.com/api/jobs")
    validate_safe_url("http://public-jobs-board.org")
    
    # Should Fail
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_safe_url("http://localhost:8000")
    
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_safe_url("http://127.0.0.1/admin")
        
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_safe_url("http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/")
        
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_safe_url("ftp://example.com")
```

## internship-platform/backend/tests/test_api_auth.py

```python
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from app.main import app

client = TestClient(app)

def test_user_registration_and_login():
    # Register test user
    reg_payload = {
        "email": "testsubscriber@example.com",
        "password": "Password123!"
    }
    response = client.post("/api/v1/auth/register", json=reg_payload)
    assert response.status_code in (200, 201, 400)  # 400 if user already exists

    # Login test user
    login_payload = {
        "username": "testsubscriber@example.com",
        "password": "Password123!"
    }
    response = client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", data=login_payload)
    assert response.status_code == 200
    token_data = response.json()
    assert "access_token" in token_data
    assert token_data["token_type"] == "bearer"
```

## internship-platform/backend/tests/test_celery_idempotency.py

```python
import pytest
from unittest.mock import patch
from app.models.scraping import ScrapingSource
from app.services.ingestion import process_and_upsert_offer
from app.tasks.scraping_tasks import scrape_source_task

@patch("app.tasks.scraping_tasks.match_new_offer_task.delay")
def test_rediscovering_existing_offer_prevents_duplicate_notifications(mock_matcher_delay, db_session):
    """
    Ensures that processing an offer a second time identifies it as 'updated' or 'unchanged',
    and explicitly does not trigger the downstream Celery matcher task.
    """
    # 1. Setup Source & Payload
    source = ScrapingSource(name="Test Source", slug="test_source", base_url="https://test.com")
    db_session.add(source)
    db_session.commit()

    raw_item = {
        "canonical_url": "https://test.com/job-100",
        "fingerprint": "hash123",
        "title": "Initial Title",
        "company": "Company A"
    }

    # 2. First Ingestion (Creates New Record)
    offer, is_new, is_updated = process_and_upsert_offer(db_session, source, raw_item)
    assert is_new is True
    assert is_updated is False
    
    # Manually simulate what scraping_tasks.py does on 'is_new == True'
    if is_new:
        mock_matcher_delay(str(offer.id))
        
    assert mock_matcher_delay.call_count == 1

    # 3. Second Ingestion with Minor Update (Title change)
    raw_item["title"] = "Updated Title"
    offer2, is_new2, is_updated2 = process_and_upsert_offer(db_session, source, raw_item)
    
    assert is_new2 is False
    assert is_updated2 is True
    
    # 4. Third Ingestion with Identical Data (Unchanged)
    offer3, is_new3, is_updated3 = process_and_upsert_offer(db_session, source, raw_item)
    
    assert is_new3 is False
    assert is_updated3 is False

    # Verify downstream Celery task was ONLY called once for the initial discovery
    if is_new2 or is_new3:
        mock_matcher_delay("should_fail")
        
    assert mock_matcher_delay.call_count == 1
```

## internship-platform/backend/tests/test_e2e_workflow.py

```python
import pytest
from unittest.mock import patch
from app.core.db import SessionLocal
from app.models.user import User, UserRole
from app.models.preference import Preference, AlertFrequency
from app.models.scraping import ScrapingSource
from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer
from app.models.match import OfferMatch
from app.models.notification import NotificationLog
from app.services.ingestion import process_and_upsert_offer
from app.tasks.matcher import match_new_offer_task
from app.tasks.notifications import queue_match_notification_task

@patch("app.tasks.notifications.get_email_provider")
def test_full_lifecycle_ingestion_to_notification(mock_get_provider):
    """
    E2E Scenario:
    1. Loads subscriber with saved preferences.
    2. Ingests a new synthetic PFE internship.
    3. Confirms offer is saved and relevance is calculated.
    4. Creates match and queues exactly one email notification.
    5. Re-ingests the same offer and confirms no duplicate alert.
    6. Tests an irrelevant offer and confirms it is ignored.
    """
    db = SessionLocal()
    
    try:
        # 1. Clean Database State
        db.query(NotificationLog).delete()
        db.query(OfferMatch).delete()
        db.query(InternshipOffer).filter(InternshipOffer.fingerprint.in_(["e2e_pfe_1", "e2e_irr_1"])).delete()
        db.query(Preference).delete()
        db.query(User).filter(User.email == "qa_e2e@example.com").delete()
        db.commit()

        # 2. Setup Subscriber & Preferences
        user = User(email="qa_e2e@example.com", hashed_password="hashed", role=UserRole.USER.value, is_active=True)
        db.add(user)
        db.commit()

        pref = Preference(
            user_id=user.id,
            target_roles=["data science", "ai"],
            skills=["python", "pytorch"],
            internship_types=["pfe"],
            min_relevance_score=60.0,
            alert_frequency=AlertFrequency.INSTANT.value
        )
        db.add(pref)
        
        source = db.query(ScrapingSource).first()
        if not source:
            source = ScrapingSource(name="E2E Source", slug="e2e", base_url="http://test.com")
            db.add(source)
            db.commit()

        # 3. Ingest Highly Relevant PFE Offer
        raw_match = {
            "source_item_id": "E2E-100",
            "canonical_url": "https://test.com/e2e-100",
            "fingerprint": "e2e_pfe_1",
            "title": "Data Science PFE Intern",
            "company": "AI Labs",
            "description": "Applying Deep Learning using Python and PyTorch.",
            "internship_type": "pfe",
            "required_skills": ["python", "pytorch"]
        }
        offer, is_new, _ = process_and_upsert_offer(db, source, raw_match)
        assert is_new is True

        # 4. Calculate Relevance & Create Match
        match_task_result = match_new_offer_task(offer.id)
        assert match_task_result["matches_created"] == 1
        
        match = db.query(OfferMatch).filter_by(offer_id=offer.id).first()
        assert match is not None
        assert match.score >= 60.0

        # 5. Queue Notification
        queue_match_notification_task(match.id)
        logs = db.query(NotificationLog).filter_by(match_id=match.id).all()
        assert len(logs) == 1
        assert logs[0].status == "SENT"
        assert mock_get_provider.return_value.send_email.call_count == 1

        # 6. Re-ingest Identical Offer (Idempotency Check)
        offer_dup, is_new_dup, _ = process_and_upsert_offer(db, source, raw_match)
        assert is_new_dup is False
        
        # Ensure attempting to notify again respects locks/logs
        queue_match_notification_task(match.id)
        logs_dup = db.query(NotificationLog).filter_by(match_id=match.id).all()
        assert len(logs_dup) == 1  # No duplicate log created
        assert mock_get_provider.return_value.send_email.call_count == 1 # Provider not called again

        # 7. Test Irrelevant Offer
        raw_irr = {
            "source_item_id": "E2E-101",
            "canonical_url": "https://test.com/e2e-101",
            "fingerprint": "e2e_irr_1",
            "title": "Marketing Summer Intern",
            "company": "Sales Corp",
            "description": "SEO and content strategy.",
            "internship_type": "summer",
            "required_skills": ["seo"]
        }
        offer_irr, is_new_irr, _ = process_and_upsert_offer(db, source, raw_irr)
        irr_task_result = match_new_offer_task(offer_irr.id)
        assert irr_task_result["matches_created"] == 0

    finally:
        # Teardown
        db.query(NotificationLog).delete()
        db.query(OfferMatch).delete()
        db.query(InternshipOffer).filter(InternshipOffer.fingerprint.in_(["e2e_pfe_1", "e2e_irr_1"])).delete()
        db.query(Preference).delete()
        db.query(User).filter(User.email == "qa_e2e@example.com").delete()
        db.commit()
        db.close()
```

## internship-platform/backend/tests/test_ingestion_idempotency.py

```python
from app.core.db import SessionLocal
from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer
from app.models.scraping import ScrapingSource
from app.services.ingestion import process_and_upsert_offer


def test_idempotent_ingestion():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        # 1. Clean up any stale records from prior runs to ensure idempotent starting state
        db.query(InternshipOffer).filter(
            InternshipOffer.fingerprint == "test_fingerprint_hash_100"
        ).delete(synchronize_session=False)
        db.commit()

        source = db.query(ScrapingSource).first()
        if not source:
            source = ScrapingSource(
                name="Test Source",
                slug="test_source",
                base_url="https://test.com"
            )
            db.add(source)
            db.commit()

        raw_item = {
            "source_item_id": "TEST-100",
            "canonical_url": "https://test.com/job-100",
            "fingerprint": "test_fingerprint_hash_100",
            "title": "Data Engineering Intern PFE",
            "company": "Data Corp",
            "location_raw": "Berlin, Germany",
            "country": "Germany",
            "workplace_type": "remote",
            "internship_type": "pfe",
            "description": "SQL Python and Spark",
            "required_skills": ["python", "sql", "spark"],
        }

        # First Ingestion (New Offer)
        offer1, is_new1, updated1 = process_and_upsert_offer(db, source, raw_item)
        assert is_new1 is True

        # Second Ingestion (Duplicate Offer - Idempotency Check)
        offer2, is_new2, updated2 = process_and_upsert_offer(db, source, raw_item)
        assert is_new2 is False
        assert offer1.id == offer2.id

    finally:
        # Cleanup test offer after test execution
        db.query(InternshipOffer).filter(
            InternshipOffer.fingerprint == "test_fingerprint_hash_100"
        ).delete(synchronize_session=False)
        db.commit()
        db.close()
```

## internship-platform/backend/tests/test_matcher.py

```python
"""
Tests for the Internship Relevance and Ranking Engine.
Covers exact/partial skills, locations, PFE constraints, exclusions, missing data,
duplicate prevention and rematching.
"""
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.core.db import SessionLocal
from app.models import InternshipOffer, OfferMatch, Preference, ScrapingSource, User, UserRole
from app.services.matcher import calculate_match_score
from app.tasks.matcher import match_new_offer_task, rematch_subscriber_preferences_task


def test_exact_skill_matches():
    offer = SimpleNamespace(
        title="Python Backend Intern",
        normalized_title="python backend intern",
        company="Tech Corp",
        description="Looking for Python and FastApi developer",
        required_skills=["python", "fastapi"],
        country="France",
        workplace_type="remote",
        internship_type="standard",
        languages=["english"],
        location_raw="Paris, France",
    )
    pref = SimpleNamespace(
        target_roles=["backend"],
        skills=["python", "fastapi"],
        preferred_countries=["france"],
        workplace_types=["remote"],
        internship_types=[],
        preferred_languages=["english"],
        keywords=[],
        exclusion_keywords=[],
    )
    res = calculate_match_score(offer, pref)
    assert res["is_excluded"] is False
    assert res["score"] >= 80.0
    assert res["breakdown"]["skills"] == 25.0


def test_partial_skill_matches():
    offer = SimpleNamespace(
        title="Software Intern",
        required_skills=["python"],
        country="France",
    )
    pref = SimpleNamespace(
        skills=["python", "docker", "kubernetes", "react"],
        preferred_countries=["france"],
    )
    res = calculate_match_score(offer, pref)
    assert res["is_excluded"] is False
    # 1 of 4 skills matched = 25% of 25 pts = 6.25 pts
    assert res["breakdown"]["skills"] == 6.25


def test_location_and_remote_preferences():
    offer = SimpleNamespace(
        title="Data Engineer",
        country="Germany",
        workplace_type="onsite",
        location_raw="Berlin, Germany",
    )
    pref = SimpleNamespace(
        preferred_countries=["france"],
        workplace_types=["remote"],
    )
    res = calculate_match_score(offer, pref)
    assert res["breakdown"]["location"] == 0.0


def test_pfe_only_preferences():
    offer = SimpleNamespace(title="Summer Intern", internship_type="summer")
    pref = SimpleNamespace(internship_types=["pfe"])
    res = calculate_match_score(offer, pref)
    assert res["is_excluded"] is True
    assert "Required PFE" in res["exclusion_reason"]


def test_excluded_keywords():
    offer = SimpleNamespace(
        title="Senior Lead Developer Intern",
        description="Must have 10 years experience",
    )
    pref = SimpleNamespace(exclusion_keywords=["senior", "lead"])
    res = calculate_match_score(offer, pref)
    assert res["is_excluded"] is True
    assert "negative keyword" in res["exclusion_reason"]


def test_missing_data_safety():
    res = calculate_match_score(SimpleNamespace(), SimpleNamespace())
    assert res["is_excluded"] is False
    assert 0.0 <= res["score"] <= 100.0


def test_duplicate_prevention_and_preference_update_db():
    # The conftest fixtures give every test an empty, isolated *_test database.
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        source = ScrapingSource(name="t", slug="t", base_url="https://t.example")
        db.add(source)
        db.commit()

        offer = InternshipOffer(
            source_id=source.id,
            canonical_url="https://t.example/1",
            fingerprint="fp1",
            title="Synthetic AI Intern",
            normalized_title="synthetic ai intern",
            company="Co",
            description="Synthetic python project",
            required_skills=["python"],
            internship_type="pfe",
        )
        user = User(email="matcher_test@example.com", hashed_password="pw", role=UserRole.SUBSCRIBER.value)
        db.add_all([offer, user])
        db.commit()

        pref = Preference(
            user_id=user.id,
            target_roles=["ai"],
            skills=["python"],
            min_relevance_score=40.0,
        )
        db.add(pref)
        db.commit()
        offer_id, user_id = offer.id, user.id

        # First run creates one match
        match_new_offer_task(offer_id)
        matches = db.query(OfferMatch).filter_by(user_id=user_id, offer_id=offer_id).all()
        assert len(matches) == 1

        # Second run must not create a duplicate
        match_new_offer_task(offer_id)
        assert db.query(OfferMatch).filter_by(user_id=user_id, offer_id=offer_id).count() == 1

        # Preference change + rematch: offer contains "synthetic", so the match disappears
        pref.exclusion_keywords = ["synthetic"]
        db.commit()
        rematch_subscriber_preferences_task(user_id)
        assert db.query(OfferMatch).filter_by(user_id=user_id, offer_id=offer_id).count() == 0
    finally:
        db.close()
```

## internship-platform/backend/tests/test_notifications.py

```python
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from app.core.db import SessionLocal
from app.models import (
    AlertFrequency,
    InternshipOffer,
    NotificationLog,
    OfferMatch,
    Preference,
    ScrapingSource,
    User,
    UserRole,
)
from app.services.email.templates import generate_match_email
from app.tasks.notifications import process_daily_digests, queue_match_notification_task


def _setup(db, email: str, frequency: str):
    source = ScrapingSource(name="t", slug="t", base_url="https://t.example")
    db.add(source)
    db.commit()

    user = User(email=email, hashed_password="pw", role=UserRole.USER.value)
    db.add(user)
    db.commit()

    db.add(Preference(user_id=user.id, alert_frequency=frequency))
    offer = InternshipOffer(
        source_id=source.id,
        canonical_url="http://i1",
        fingerprint="f1",
        title="Offer",
        company="Co",
    )
    db.add(offer)
    db.commit()

    match = OfferMatch(user_id=user.id, offer_id=offer.id, score=90.0)
    db.add(match)
    db.commit()
    return match.id


def test_generate_match_email_rendering():
    user = User(id=1, email="test@example.com")
    offer = InternshipOffer(
        title="AI Engineer", company="Tech", country="France",
        workplace_type="remote", internship_type="pfe", canonical_url="http://test.com",
    )
    match = OfferMatch(user=user, offer=offer, score=95.0, reasons=["Role match"])

    payload = generate_match_email(user, [match])

    assert "AI Engineer" in payload["subject"]
    assert "Tech" in payload["html_content"]
    assert "95.0%" in payload["text_content"]
    assert "token=" in payload["html_content"]  # secure unsubscribe link present


def test_email_escapes_scraped_html():
    user = User(id=1, email="test@example.com")
    offer = InternshipOffer(title="<script>x</script>", company="Co", canonical_url="http://t.com")
    match = OfferMatch(user=user, offer=offer, score=50.0, reasons=[])

    payload = generate_match_email(user, [match])

    assert "<script>" not in payload["html_content"]


@patch("app.tasks.notifications.get_email_provider")
def test_idempotent_instant_notification(mock_get_provider):
    provider = MagicMock()
    mock_get_provider.return_value = provider
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        match_id = _setup(db, "instant@example.com", AlertFrequency.INSTANT.value)

        # Call 1: sends
        queue_match_notification_task(match_id)
        logs = db.query(NotificationLog).filter_by(match_id=match_id).all()
        assert len(logs) == 1
        assert logs[0].status == "SENT"
        assert provider.send_email.call_count == 1

        # Call 2: suppressed by the idempotency check
        queue_match_notification_task(match_id)
        assert db.query(NotificationLog).filter_by(match_id=match_id).count() == 1
        assert provider.send_email.call_count == 1
    finally:
        db.close()


@patch("app.tasks.notifications.get_email_provider")
def test_daily_digest_sends_once(mock_get_provider):
    provider = MagicMock()
    mock_get_provider.return_value = provider
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        match_id = _setup(db, "daily@example.com", AlertFrequency.DAILY.value)

        process_daily_digests()
        logs = db.query(NotificationLog).filter_by(match_id=match_id).all()
        assert len(logs) == 1
        assert logs[0].status == "SENT"
        assert provider.send_email.call_count == 1

        process_daily_digests()  # nothing new to send
        assert provider.send_email.call_count == 1
    finally:
        db.close()
```

## internship-platform/backend/tests/test_workflow_idempotency.py

```python
import json
from unittest.mock import patch

import pytest

from app.models.offer import InternshipOffer
from app.models.scraping import ScrapingSource
from app.tasks.scraping_tasks import scrape_source_task


@pytest.fixture
def mock_scrapy_results():
    return [{
        "source_item_id": "TEST-100",
        "canonical_url": "https://test.com/job-100",
        "fingerprint": "test_fingerprint_hash_100",
        "title": "Software Engineering Intern",
        "company": "Tech Corp",
        "required_skills": ["python", "docker"],
    }]


@patch("app.tasks.scraping_tasks.match_new_offer_task.delay")
@patch("subprocess.run")
def test_rediscovering_offer_prevents_duplicate_notifications(
    mock_subprocess_run, mock_match_delay, mock_scrapy_results, db_session
):
    """Scraping the same data twice must trigger the matcher only once.
    Needs Redis (the scrape lock), so run it inside docker compose."""

    source = ScrapingSource(name="Test Source", slug="test_source", base_url="https://test.com")
    db_session.add(source)
    db_session.commit()
    source_id = source.id

    def side_effect_run(*args, **kwargs):
        # Write the JSON file that Scrapy would have produced.
        output_file = args[0][args[0].index("-O") + 1]
        with open(output_file, "w") as f:
            json.dump(mock_scrapy_results, f)

        class CompletedProcessMock:
            returncode = 0
            stderr = ""

        return CompletedProcessMock()

    mock_subprocess_run.side_effect = side_effect_run

    # Run 1: offer is new
    result_run_1 = scrape_source_task(source_id)
    assert result_run_1["metrics"]["new"] == 1
    assert result_run_1["metrics"]["unchanged"] == 0
    assert mock_match_delay.call_count == 1

    # Run 2: identical data scraped again
    result_run_2 = scrape_source_task(source_id)
    assert result_run_2["metrics"]["new"] == 0
    assert result_run_2["metrics"]["unchanged"] == 1
    assert mock_match_delay.call_count == 1  # matcher NOT triggered again

    offers = db_session.query(InternshipOffer).filter_by(fingerprint="test_fingerprint_hash_100").all()
    assert len(offers) == 1
```

```yaml
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:15-alpine
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER:-postgres}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:-postgres}
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB:-internship_radar}
    ports: ["127.0.0.1:5432:5432"]
    volumes: [postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data]
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U ${POSTGRES_USER:-postgres}"]
      interval: 5s
      retries: 10

  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports: ["127.0.0.1:6379:6379"]
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "redis-cli", "ping"]
      interval: 5s
      retries: 10

  backend: &backend
    build: ./backend
    restart: unless-stopped
    env_file: .env
    ports: ["8000:8000"]
    depends_on:
      postgres: {condition: service_healthy}
      redis: {condition: service_healthy}
    volumes: ["./backend:/app", "./scraper:/app/scraper"]

  worker:
    <<: *backend
    ports: []
    command: celery -A app.core.celery_app worker --loglevel=info

  beat:
    <<: *backend
    ports: []
    command: celery -A app.core.celery_app beat --loglevel=info --schedule /tmp/celerybeat-schedule

  frontend:
    build: ./frontend
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports: ["3000:3000"]
    environment:
      API_URL: http://backend:8000/api/v1
      NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL: http://localhost:8000/api/v1
    depends_on: [backend]
    volumes: ["./frontend:/app", "/app/node_modules", "/app/.next"]

volumes:
  postgres_data:
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/app/(auth)/login/page.tsx

```tsx
'use client'

import { useFormState, useFormStatus } from 'react-dom';
import { loginAction } from '@/app/actions/auth';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Briefcase } from 'lucide-react';

function SubmitButton() {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <button
      type="submit"
      disabled={pending}
      className="w-full flex justify-center py-2.5 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-md shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50"
    >
      {pending ? 'Signing in...' : 'Sign In'}
    </button>
  );
}

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [state, formAction] = useFormState(loginAction, null);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[80vh] flex items-center justify-center py-12 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="max-w-md w-full space-y-8 bg-white p-8 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
        <div className="text-center">
          <Briefcase className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-blue-600" />
          <h2 className="mt-6 text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">Welcome back</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
            Sign in to manage your internship alerts
          </p>
        </div>
        <form className="mt-8 space-y-6" action={formAction}>
          {state?.error && (
            <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-600 px-4 py-3 rounded-md text-sm">
              {state.error}
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1" htmlFor="email">Email address</label>
              <input
                id="email"
                name="email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                required
                className="appearance-none block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-md shadow-sm placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 sm:text-sm"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1" htmlFor="password">Password</label>
              <input
                id="password"
                name="password"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="current-password"
                required
                className="appearance-none block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-md shadow-sm placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 sm:text-sm"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <SubmitButton />
          
          <div className="text-center mt-4">
            <Link href="/register" className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-500">
              Don't have an account? Sign up
            </Link>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/app/(auth)/register/page.tsx

```tsx
'use client'

import { useFormState, useFormStatus } from 'react-dom';
import { registerAction } from '@/app/actions/auth';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Briefcase } from 'lucide-react';

function SubmitButton() {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return (
    <button
      type="submit"
      disabled={pending}
      className="w-full flex justify-center py-2.5 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-md shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50"
    >
      {pending ? 'Creating account...' : 'Create account'}
    </button>
  );
}

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const [state, formAction] = useFormState(registerAction, null);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[80vh] flex items-center justify-center py-12 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="max-w-md w-full space-y-8 bg-white p-8 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl shadow-sm">

          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
            Get alerts when matching internships appear
          </p>
        </div>
        <form className="mt-8 space-y-6" action={formAction}>
          {state?.error && (
            <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-600 px-4 py-3 rounded-md text-sm">
              {state.error}
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1" htmlFor="email">Email address</label>
              <input
                id="email"
                name="email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                required
                className="appearance-none block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-md shadow-sm placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 sm:text-sm"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1" htmlFor="password">Password (min. 8 characters)</label>
              <input
                id="password"
                name="password"
                type="password"
                autoComplete="new-password"
                minLength={8}
                required
                className="appearance-none block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-md shadow-sm placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 sm:text-sm"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <SubmitButton />

          <div className="text-center mt-4">
            <Link href="/login" className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-500">
              Already have an account? Sign in
            </Link>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/app/actions/auth.ts

```typescript
'use server'

import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';
import { API_URL } from '@/lib/api-client';

export type FormState = { error?: string; ok?: boolean } | null;

function errorText(body: any, fallback: string) {
  const d = body?.detail;
  if (typeof d === 'string') return d;
  if (Array.isArray(d)) return d.map((e: any) => e.msg).join('; ');
  return fallback;
}

function setSession(token: string) {
  cookies().set('token', token, {
    httpOnly: true, secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
    sameSite: 'lax', maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24, path: '/',
  });
}

export async function loginAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  params.append('username', String(formData.get('email') ?? ''));
  params.append('password', String(formData.get('password') ?? ''));
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/auth/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    body: params.toString(),
  });
  if (!res.ok) return { error: errorText(await res.json().catch(() => ({})), 'Login failed') };
  setSession((await res.json()).access_token);
  redirect('/dashboard');
}

export async function registerAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/auth/register`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email: formData.get('email'), password: formData.get('password') }),
  });
  if (!res.ok) return { error: errorText(await res.json().catch(() => ({})), 'Registration failed') };
  setSession((await res.json()).access_token);
  redirect('/dashboard/preferences');
}

export async function logoutAction() {
  cookies().delete('token');
  redirect('/login');
}

const list = (v: FormDataEntryValue | null) =>
  String(v ?? '').split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);

export async function savePreferencesAction(_prev: FormState, formData: FormData): Promise<FormState> {
  const token = cookies().get('token')?.value;
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/preferences`, {
    method: 'PUT',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      target_roles: list(formData.get('target_roles')),
      preferred_countries: list(formData.get('preferred_countries')),
      skills: list(formData.get('skills')),
      alert_frequency: formData.get('alert_frequency'),
      min_relevance_score: Number(formData.get('min_relevance_score') ?? 50),
    }),
  });
  if (!res.ok) return { error: errorText(await res.json().catch(() => ({})), 'Save failed') };
  revalidatePath('/dashboard/preferences');
  return { ok: true };
}

export async function triggerScrapeAction(sourceId: number): Promise<FormState> {
  const token = cookies().get('token')?.value;
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/admin/sources/${sourceId}/trigger`, {
    method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
  });
  if (!res.ok) return { error: errorText(await res.json().catch(() => ({})), 'Trigger failed') };
  revalidatePath('/dashboard/admin');
  return { ok: true };
}
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/app/dashboard/admin/page.tsx

```tsx
import { fetchAPI } from '@/lib/api-client';
import { DashboardKPIs, ScrapingSource, ScrapingRun } from '@/types/admin';
import TriggerButton from '@/components/trigger-button';
import { Activity, Database, Users, AlertTriangle, RefreshCw, CheckCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default async function AdminDashboard() {
  let kpis: DashboardKPIs | null = null;
  let sources: ScrapingSource[] = [];
  let runs: ScrapingRun[] = [];
  let error: string | null = null;

  try {
    [kpis, sources, runs] = await Promise.all([
      fetchAPI<DashboardKPIs>('/admin/kpis'),
      fetchAPI<ScrapingSource[]>('/admin/sources'),
      fetchAPI<ScrapingRun[]>('/admin/runs?limit=10'),
    ]);
  } catch (err: any) {
    error = err.message || 'Failed to load administrative data. Are you an admin?';
  }

  if (error || !kpis) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-center text-red-600 bg-red-50 m-4 rounded-lg">{error}</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10 space-y-10">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Platform Command Center</h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2">Database-backed metrics, scraping controls, and system health.</p>
      </div>

      {/* KPIs */}
      <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
          <Database className="h-10 w-10 text-blue-500 bg-blue-50 p-2 rounded-lg" />
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">Active Opportunities</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{kpis.total_active_offers}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
          <Users className="h-10 w-10 text-green-500 bg-green-50 p-2 rounded-lg" />
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">Active Subscribers</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{kpis.active_subscribers}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
          <Activity className="h-10 w-10 text-purple-500 bg-purple-50 p-2 rounded-lg" />
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">7-Day Discovery</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">+{kpis.recent_offers_7d}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Sources */}
      <section className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-6 py-5 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-800">Scraping Sources</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
          {sources.map((source) => (
            <div key={source.id} className="p-6 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{source.name}</h3>
                  <span
                    className={`px-2 py-0.5 text-xs rounded-full font-medium ${
                      source.is_active ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {source.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Disabled'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">{source.base_url}</p>
              </div>
              <TriggerButton sourceId={source.id} disabled={!source.is_active} />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Recent runs */}
      <section className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-6 py-5 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-800">Recent Scraping Execution History</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-slate-500">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-6 py-3 font-medium">Source</th>
                <th className="px-6 py-3 font-medium">Status</th>
                <th className="px-6 py-3 font-medium">Extracted (New)</th>
                <th className="px-6 py-3 font-medium">Started At</th>
                <th className="px-6 py-3 font-medium">Duration</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
              {runs.map((run) => (
                <tr key={run.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors">
                  <td className="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-slate-900">{run.source_name}</td>
                  <td className="px-6 py-4">
                    {run.status === 'SUCCESS' ? (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-green-600 font-medium">
                        <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" /> Success
                      </span>
                    ) : run.status === 'FAILED' ? (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-red-600 font-medium">
                        <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" /> Failed
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-blue-600 font-medium">
                        <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4" /> Pending
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-6 py-4 text-slate-600">
                    {run.items_found} <span className="text-green-600 font-medium">(+{run.items_new})</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-6 py-4 text-slate-500">{new Date(run.started_at).toLocaleString()}</td>
                  <td className="px-6 py-4 text-slate-500">
                    {run.duration_seconds != null ? `${run.duration_seconds.toFixed(1)}s` : '-'}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx

```tsx
import Link from 'next/link';
import { fetchAPI } from '@/lib/api-client';

interface MatchRow { id: number; score: number; reasons: string[];
  offer: { id: number; title: string; company: string; country: string; canonical_url: string } }

export default async function Dashboard() {
  let matches: MatchRow[] = [];
  let error: string | null = null;
  try { matches = await fetchAPI<MatchRow[]>('/matches'); } catch (e: any) { error = e.message; }
  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-10 space-y-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Your matches</h1>
        <Link href="/dashboard/preferences" className="text-blue-600 text-sm">Edit preferences</Link>
      </div>
      {error && <p className="text-red-600">{error}</p>}
      {!error && matches.length === 0 && <p className="text-slate-500">No matches yet.</p>}
      {matches.map((m) => (
        <a key={m.id} href={m.offer.canonical_url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"
           className="block bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-5 hover:border-blue-300">
          <div className="flex justify-between">
            <div><h2 className="font-semibold">{m.offer.title}</h2>
              <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm">{m.offer.company} · {m.offer.country}</p></div>
            <span className="text-blue-700 font-bold">{m.score.toFixed(0)}%</span>
          </div>
          {m.reasons[0] && <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{m.reasons[0]}</p>}
        </a>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/app/offers/page.tsx

```tsx
import { fetchAPI } from '@/lib/api-client';
import { InternshipOffer } from '@/types';
import { MapPin, Briefcase, Calendar, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';

export default async function OffersPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: { search?: string; country?: string };
}) {
  const query = new URLSearchParams();
  if (searchParams.search) query.append('search', searchParams.search);
  if (searchParams.country) query.append('country', searchParams.country);

  let offers: InternshipOffer[] = [];
  try {
    offers = await fetchAPI<InternshipOffer[]>(`/offers?${query.toString()}`);
  } catch (error) {
    return <div className="text-center py-20 text-red-600">Failed to load offers. Please try again later.</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-10">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">Internship Discovery</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mt-1">Showing {offers.length} recent opportunities matching your criteria.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {offers.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-12 text-center">
          <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-slate-900">No offers found</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2">Try adjusting your search criteria or removing filters.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {offers.map((offer) => (
            <a
              key={offer.id}
              href={offer.canonical_url}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="block group"
            >
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-6 hover:shadow-md hover:border-blue-300 transition-all">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-blue-600 transition-colors">
                      {offer.title}
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-slate-600 font-medium mt-1">{offer.company}</p>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 mt-4 text-sm text-slate-500">
                      {offer.location_raw && (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <MapPin className="h-4 w-4" /> {offer.location_raw}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {offer.workplace_type && (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Briefcase className="h-4 w-4" /> {offer.workplace_type.replace('_', ' ')}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {offer.published_at && (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Calendar className="h-4 w-4" /> {new Date(offer.published_at).toLocaleDateString()}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {offer.internship_type && (
                        <span className="bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-semibold uppercase">
                          {offer.internship_type}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {(offer.required_skills ?? []).length > 0 && (
                      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                        {(offer.required_skills ?? []).map((skill) => (
                          <span key={skill} className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-medium">
                            {skill}
                          </span>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="hidden md:flex items-center justify-center h-10 w-10 bg-slate-50 rounded-full group-hover:bg-blue-50 transition-colors">
                    <ChevronRight className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-blue-600" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </a>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/components/navigation.tsx

```tsx
import Link from 'next/link';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { Briefcase, User, LogOut } from 'lucide-react';
import { logoutAction } from '@/app/actions/auth';

export default function Navigation() {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const isAuthenticated = cookieStore.has('token');

  return (
    <nav className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200 sticky top-0 z-50">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex justify-between h-16">
          <div className="flex items-center">
            <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Briefcase className="h-6 w-6 text-blue-600" />
              <span className="font-bold text-xl text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
                InternshipRadar<span className="text-blue-600">.ai</span>
              </span>
            </Link>
            <div className="hidden md:flex ml-10 space-x-8">
              <Link href="/offers" className="text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
                Discover Offers
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
            {isAuthenticated ? (
              <>
                <Link href="/dashboard" className="text-slate-600 hover:text-blue-600 p-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
                  <User className="h-4 w-4" /> Dashboard
                </Link>
                <form action={logoutAction}>
                  <button type="submit" className="text-slate-600 hover:text-red-600 p-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
                    <LogOut className="h-4 w-4" /> Sign Out
                  </button>
                </form>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Link href="/login" className="text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors">
                  Sign In
                </Link>
                <Link href="/register" className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors shadow-sm">
                  Get Alerts
                </Link>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client'
import { useFormState, useFormStatus } from 'react-dom';
import { savePreferencesAction } from '@/app/actions/auth';
import { Preference } from '@/types';

function Save() {
  const { pending } = useFormStatus();
  return <button type="submit" disabled={pending}
    className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-md text-sm disabled:opacity-50">
    {pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Save Preferences'}</button>;
}

const input = 'w-full border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm';

export default function PreferencesForm({ pref }: { pref: Preference }) {
  const [state, action] = useFormState(savePreferencesAction, null);
  return (
    <form action={action} className="space-y-6 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-8">
      {state?.error && <p className="text-red-600 text-sm">{state.error}</p>}
      {state?.ok && <p className="text-green-600 text-sm">Saved.</p>}
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Target roles (comma separated)
        <input name="target_roles" defaultValue={pref.target_roles.join(', ')} className={input} /></label>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Preferred countries
        <input name="preferred_countries" defaultValue={pref.preferred_countries.join(', ')} className={input} /></label>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Skills
        <textarea name="skills" rows={3} defaultValue={pref.skills.join(', ')} className={input} /></label>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Alert frequency
        <select name="alert_frequency" defaultValue={pref.alert_frequency} className={input}>
          <option value="INSTANT">Real-time</option><option value="DAILY">Daily digest</option>
          <option value="WEEKLY">Weekly (not implemented yet)</option><option value="OFF">Paused</option>
        </select></label>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Minimum match score (0-100)
        <input name="min_relevance_score" type="number" min={0} max={100}
          defaultValue={pref.min_relevance_score} className={input} /></label>
      <div className="flex justify-end"><Save /></div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/components/trigger-button.tsx

```tsx
'use client'

import { useState, useTransition } from 'react';
import { Play } from 'lucide-react';
import { triggerScrapeAction } from '@/app/actions/auth';

export default function TriggerButton({ sourceId, disabled }: { sourceId: number; disabled?: boolean }) {
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const onClick = () => {
    setMessage(null);
    startTransition(async () => {
      const result = await triggerScrapeAction(sourceId);
      setMessage(result?.error ? `Failed: ${result.error}` : 'Scrape queued.');
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
      {message && <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">{message}</span>}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onClick}
        disabled={disabled || pending}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        <Play className="h-4 w-4" /> {pending ? 'Queuing...' : 'Force Run'}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## internship-platform/frontend/src/lib/api-client.ts

```typescript
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

export const API_URL =
  process.env.API_URL || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || 'http://localhost:8000/api/v1';

export async function fetchAPI<T>(endpoint: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  const token = cookies().get('token')?.value;
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    ...((options.headers as Record<string, string>) || {}),
  };
  if (token) headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;

  const init: RequestInit = { ...options, headers };
  if (!options.cache && !(options as any).next) init.cache = 'no-store';

  const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}${endpoint}`, init);
  if (!response.ok) {
    const err = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(typeof err.detail === 'string' ? err.detail : `API request failed (${response.status})`);
  }
  return response.json();
}
```

## internship-platform/scraper/internship_scraper/items.py

```python
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, HttpUrl, ConfigDict, field_serializer


class WorkplaceType(str, Enum):
    REMOTE = "remote"
    HYBRID = "hybrid"
    ON_SITE = "on_site"
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


class InternshipType(str, Enum):
    PFE = "pfe"
    SUMMER = "summer"
    OFF_GRADUATE = "off_graduate"
    GENERAL = "general"


class NormalizedInternshipOffer(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(use_enum_values=True)

    source_name: str = Field(..., description="Unique slug for the source adapter")
    source_item_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Native identifier provided by source")
    canonical_url: HttpUrl = Field(..., description="Canonical link to the listing")

    title: str = Field(..., description="Raw listing title")
    normalized_title: str = Field(..., description="Lowercased, sanitized title")
    company: str = Field(..., description="Company or organization offering the internship")

    location_raw: str = Field(..., description="Unparsed location string from source")
    city: Optional[str] = None
    country: str = Field("Europe", description="Country name or ISO code")
    workplace_type: WorkplaceType = Field(default=WorkplaceType.UNKNOWN)

    internship_type: InternshipType = Field(default=InternshipType.PFE)
    description: str = Field(..., description="Sanitized clean HTML or plain text description")
    required_skills: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Extracted AI/Data skill keywords")
    languages: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Extracted language requirements")

    published_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    application_deadline: Optional[datetime] = None
    first_seen_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=datetime.utcnow)
    last_seen_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=datetime.utcnow)

    fingerprint: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="SHA256 fingerprint for fall-back deduplication")

    @field_serializer('canonical_url')
    def serialize_url(self, url: HttpUrl, _info):
        return str(url)

    @field_serializer('published_at', 'application_deadline', 'first_seen_at', 'last_seen_at')
    def serialize_dt(self, dt: Optional[datetime], _info):
        return dt.isoformat() if dt else None
```

## internship-platform/scraper/internship_scraper/pipelines.py

```python
import hashlib
import logging
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, Union

from scrapy.exceptions import DropItem

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _getter(item):
    return lambda key: item.get(key, "") if isinstance(item, dict) else getattr(item, key, "")


class ContentDeduplicationPipeline:
    """
    Generates a conservative fallback SHA256 signature from company, title and country.
    The backend uses it to avoid storing the same offer twice.
    """

    def process_item(self, item: Union[Dict[str, Any], Any], spider) -> Union[Dict[str, Any], Any]:
        get_val = _getter(item)

        company = get_val("company")
        title = get_val("normalized_title") or get_val("title")
        country = get_val("country")

        raw_sig = f"{str(company).lower()}|{str(title).lower()}|{str(country).lower()}"
        clean_sig = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9|]", "", raw_sig)
        fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(clean_sig.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

        if isinstance(item, dict):
            item["fingerprint"] = fingerprint
        else:
            item.fingerprint = fingerprint

        return item


class KeywordFilterPipeline:
    """
    Drops listings that are not relevant to AI / Data Science / ML / PFE and
    extracts known skills into required_skills for the ones that are kept.
    """

    TARGET_KEYWORDS = {
    # English
        "artificial intelligence", "ai", "machine learning", "deep learning",
        "data science", "data engineer", "data engineering", "nlp",
        "natural language processing", "computer vision", "llm", "pfe",
        "end of studies", "end-of-studies", "master thesis", "applied ai",
        "neural network", "neural networks", "reinforcement learning", "big data",
        "data intelligence",
        # French
        "intelligence artificielle", "ia", "apprentissage automatique",
        "apprentissage profond", "apprentissage statistique", "science des données",
        "sciences des données", "ingénieur données", "réseau de neurones",
        "réseaux de neurones", "vision par ordinateur",
        "traitement automatique des langues", "traitement du langage naturel",
        "stage de fin d'études",
    }

    SKILL_VOCABULARY = {
        "python", "pytorch", "tensorflow", "scikit-learn", "sql", "spark",
        "hadoop", "docker", "kubernetes", "aws", "gcp", "azure", "fastapi",
        "mlflow", "huggingface", "transformers", "opencv", "langchain",
    }

    def process_item(self, item: Union[Dict[str, Any], Any], spider) -> Union[Dict[str, Any], Any]:
        get_val = _getter(item)

        title = get_val("title")
        description = get_val("description")
        full_text = f"{title} {description}".lower()
        full_text = full_text.replace("’", "'")

        if not any(re.search(rf"\b{re.escape(k)}\b", full_text) for k in self.TARGET_KEYWORDS):
            raise DropItem(f"Not relevant: {str(title)[:60]}")

        extracted_skills = {
            skill for skill in self.SKILL_VOCABULARY
            if re.search(rf"(?<![\w-]){re.escape(skill)}(?![\w-])", full_text)
        }

        if isinstance(item, dict):
            item["required_skills"] = sorted(extracted_skills)
        else:
            item.required_skills = sorted(extracted_skills)

        return item
```

## internship-platform/scraper/internship_scraper/spiders/abg_spider.py

```python
import logging
import math
import re
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

import scrapy

from internship_scraper.items import InternshipType, NormalizedInternshipOffer, WorkplaceType
from internship_scraper.spiders.base_spider import BaseInternshipSpider

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

PAGE_RE = re.compile(r"/recherche/page/(\d+)")


def _clean(text: Optional[str]) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", (text or "").replace("\xa0", " ")).strip()


class AbgMasterInternshipsSpider(BaseInternshipSpider):
    """ABG (Association Bernard Gregory): 'Stage master 2 / Ingénieur' offers.

    The search page loads results by AJAX from /fr/candidatOffres/recherche/page/N,
    so we request that endpoint directly, as the site's own JavaScript does.
    """

    name = "abg_master_internships"  # must equal ScrapingSource.slug
    allowed_domains = ["abg.asso.fr"]
    start_urls = ["https://www.abg.asso.fr/fr/candidatOffres"]  # sets the session cookie

    SEARCH_URL = "https://www.abg.asso.fr/fr/candidatOffres/recherche/page/{page}"
    MAX_PAGES = 100
    WANTED_TYPE = "stage"  # matches 'Stage master 2 / Ingénieur'

    def parse(self, response):
        yield self._search_request(1)

    def _search_request(self, page: int) -> scrapy.Request:
        return scrapy.Request(
            self.SEARCH_URL.format(page=page),
            method="POST",
            headers={
                "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest",
                "Referer": "https://www.abg.asso.fr/fr/candidatOffres",
            },
            callback=self.parse_listing,
            cb_kwargs={"page": page},
            dont_filter=True,
        )

    def parse_listing(self, response, page: int = 1):
        for node in response.css("div.it_offre"):
            try:
                item = self._parse_offer(node, response)
            except Exception as exc:  # one bad listing must not stop the crawl
                self.stats_summary["errors"] += 1
                logger.warning("Skipping unparsable ABG listing: %s", exc)
                continue
            if item is not None:
                self.stats_summary["items_scraped"] += 1
                yield item

        if page == 1:
            pages = [int(n) for n in PAGE_RE.findall(response.text)]
            last = min(max(pages, default=1), self.MAX_PAGES)
            for next_page in range(2, last + 1):
                yield self._search_request(next_page)

    def _parse_offer(self, node, response) -> Optional[dict]:
        type_label = _clean(node.css("div.ligne_infos div.type::text").get()).lower()
        if self.WANTED_TYPE not in type_label:
            return None  # job or PhD thesis

        title = _clean(node.css("h2 a::text").get())
        href = node.css("h2 a::attr(href)").get()
        if not title or not href:
            return None

        company = _clean(" ".join(node.xpath(
            './/div[contains(@class,"societe")]//text()[not(ancestor::span)]').getall()))
        ref = _clean(node.css("div.ref::text").get()).replace("Ref.", "").strip()

        location = _clean(" ".join(node.css("div.adresse ::text").getall())).replace(" ,", ",")
        parts = [p.strip() for p in location.split(",") if p.strip()]
        country = parts[-1] if len(parts) > 1 else "France"

        snippet = _clean(" ".join(node.css("div.text ::text").getall())).rstrip(". ")
        keywords = _clean(" ".join(node.xpath(
            './/div[@class="mots"]//text()[not(ancestor::label)]').getall()))
        fields = ", ".join(
            _clean(t) for t in node.xpath(
                './/div[contains(@class,"expertises")]//text()'
                '[not(ancestor::label) and not(ancestor::span)]').getall()
            if _clean(t))

        description = snippet
        if keywords:
            description += f". Mots clés : {keywords}"
        if fields:
            description += f". Domaines : {fields}"

        published = None
        raw_date = _clean(node.css("div.l_date::text").get())
        if raw_date:
            try:
                published = datetime.strptime(raw_date, "%d/%m/%Y")
            except ValueError:
                pass

        offer = NormalizedInternshipOffer(
            source_name=self.name,
            source_item_id=ref or None,
            canonical_url=response.urljoin(href),
            title=title,
            normalized_title=title.lower(),
            company=company or "Non précisé",
            location_raw=location or "France",
            country=country,
            workplace_type=WorkplaceType.UNKNOWN,
            internship_type=InternshipType.PFE,  # 'Stage master 2 / Ingénieur'
            description=description or title,
            published_at=published,
        )
        return offer.model_dump()
```

## internship-platform/scraper/internship_scraper/spiders/base_spider.py

```python
import scrapy
from typing import Dict, Any, Generator
from datetime import datetime
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup


class BaseInternshipSpider(scrapy.Spider):
    """
    Base Spider enforcing compliance, logging, and unified text parsing methods.
    """
    
    custom_settings: Dict[str, Any] = {
        'ROBOTSTXT_OBEY': True,
        'CONCURRENT_REQUESTS_PER_DOMAIN': 2,
        'DOWNLOAD_DELAY': 2.0,
        'AUTOTHROTTLE_ENABLED': True,
        'AUTOTHROTTLE_START_DELAY': 2.0,
        'AUTOTHROTTLE_MAX_DELAY': 10.0,
        'HTTPCACHE_ENABLED': False,
    }

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.stats_summary = {
            "source": self.name,
            "start_time": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
            "items_scraped": 0,
            "errors": 0
        }

    def parse(self, response: scrapy.http.Response) -> Generator[Dict[str, Any], None, None]:
        raise NotImplementedError("Subclasses must implement parse()")

    def clean_html(self, raw_html: str) -> str:
        if not raw_html:
            return ""
        soup = BeautifulSoup(raw_html, "html.parser")
        for element in soup(["script", "style", "iframe"]):
            element.decompose()
        return soup.get_text(separator=" ", strip=True)

    def parse_workplace_type(self, text: str) -> str:
        text_lower = text.lower()
        if "remote" in text_lower or "teletravail" in text_lower:
            return "remote"
        elif "hybrid" in text_lower or "hybride" in text_lower:
            return "hybrid"
        elif "on-site" in text_lower or "sur site" in text_lower or "onsite" in text_lower:
            return "on_site"
        return "unknown"
```

## internship-platform/scraper/internship_scraper/spiders/europa_jobs_spider.py

```python
import scrapy
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from dateutil import parser as date_parser
from internship_scraper.spiders.base_spider import BaseInternshipSpider
from internship_scraper.items import NormalizedInternshipOffer, WorkplaceType, InternshipType


class EuropaJobsRSSSpider(BaseInternshipSpider):
    name = "europa_jobs_rss"
    allowed_domains = ["europa.eu", "ec.europa.eu"]
    # Verified active feed URL
    start_urls = ["https://ec.europa.eu/commission/presscorner/api/rss?language=en"]

    def parse(self, response):
        """Parse compliant XML/RSS feed entries."""
        try:
            root = ET.fromstring(response.body)
        except ET.ParseError:
            self.logger.error("Failed to parse XML payload from feed.")
            return

        channel = root.find("channel")
        if channel is None:
            return

        for item in channel.findall("item"):
            title = item.findtext("title", default="").strip()
            link = item.findtext("link", default="").strip()
            description_raw = item.findtext("description", default="").strip()
            pub_date_str = item.findtext("pubDate", default="")

            pub_date = None
            if pub_date_str:
                try:
                    pub_date = date_parser.parse(pub_date_str)
                except Exception:
                    pub_date = None

            cleaned_desc = self.clean_html(description_raw)

            offer = NormalizedInternshipOffer(
                source_name=self.name,
                source_item_id=item.findtext("guid", default=link),
                canonical_url=link,
                title=title,
                normalized_title=title.lower(),
                company="European Commission",
                location_raw="Brussels, Europe",
                country="Europe",
                workplace_type=WorkplaceType(self.parse_workplace_type(cleaned_desc)),
                internship_type=InternshipType.PFE if "pfe" in cleaned_desc.lower() or "master" in cleaned_desc.lower() else InternshipType.GENERAL,
                description=cleaned_desc or title,
                published_at=pub_date
            )

            self.stats_summary["items_scraped"] += 1
            yield offer.model_dump()
```

```html
	<meta name="title" content=" 140 - Integration of neural networks in industrial process control adaptation logic of a run-out table controller for hot strip mills" />
<meta name="robots" content="index, follow" />
<meta name="description" content="Reference Offre 140

&amp;nbsp;

The hot strip mill is the process where a hot-rolled coil is produced from a slab after being rolled multiple times in the rolling stands and then cooled with water using a run-out table to control the shape and the mechanical properties of the strip.
A run-out table is key process in hot rolling composed of water jets controlled by multiple valves to reach a desired temperature along the length of the product. R&amp;amp;D developed an industrial water-cooling controller for run-out table based on temperature and metallurgical predictions.

Temperature control is a key challenge in such manufacturing processes, where product quality strongly depends on the ability to accurately predict and regulate thermal behaviour under a wide range of operating conditions.

Current run out table control systems rely on physical and mathematical models to predict process behaviour and determine optimal operating parameters. While these models provide robust and accurate predictions in most situations, their performance may decrease when operating outside their calibration domain or when confronted with atypical process conditions.

The objective of this internship is to investigate and develop a hybrid modelling approach combining physical models and neural networks to improve the adaptation capabilities of the run out table controller. The work will consist in leveraging historical process data and existing adaptation mechanisms to enhance prediction accuracy and controller performance while maintaining the robustness required for industrial deployment.

The activities of the mission will include:

- Understanding the industrial process and the existing control strategy.
- Reviewing state-of-the-art machine learning approaches for industrial applications.
- Analysing historical process and production data.
- Developing and training neural network models in Python.
- Reusing and extending existing adaptation algorithms.
- Integrating machine learning models into the control adaptation workflow.
- Evaluating model performance through offline simulations and industrial case studies.
- Comparing conventional physical-model-based and hybrid AI-based approaches.
- Documenting developments and presenting results to the R&amp;amp;D team.

The training will be provided by a team of technicians and engineers in Maizi&amp;egrave;res lab. Some contacts with ArcelorMittal Hot Strip Mills could be requested to gather information.

The language of everyday communication is in French but reports and presentations will be in English. The internship offers the opportunity to combine data science, software development and industrial process understanding on a real-world application.
" />

	<div class="offrePage">

	
	<h1>
		 140 - Integration of neural networks in industrial process control adaptation logic of a run-out table controller for hot strip mills	</h1>

	<table class="tab_offre_infos">
		<tr>
			<td>
				<label>Réf</label>
				<strong>ABG-140502</strong>
			</td>
			<td>
				Stage master 2 / Ingénieur			</td>
						<td>
				<label>
					Durée				</label>
					6					mois			</td>
						<td>
				<label>
					Salaire net mensuel				</label>
				-			</td>
		</tr>
		<tr>
			<td>12/10/2026</td>
			<td></td>
			<td></td>
		</tr>
	</table>


	<div class="box">

			<div class="box_societe">

			
			<div class="societe">
				ArcelorMittal Global R&D Maizières			</div>

			
		</div>
	
		<div class="box_infosP">

			<div class="item">
				<label>
					Lieu de travail				</label>
				<div class="text infos">
					Metz										Grand Est					France									</div>

	</div>
	<!-- fin box -->

	<div class="desc">

			<div class="box">
			<h2>
				Établissement recruteur			</h2>

						<div class="text">
				<p><strong>Who are we</strong></p>

<p><br />
ArcelorMittal is one of the world&#39;s leading steel companies. Every day, its 154,000 employees in 60 countries help build a better world with smarter steels that consume less energy and emit less carbon.</p>

<p><br />
<strong>The Maizi&egrave;res campus</strong></p>

<p><br />
The Maizi&egrave;res campus is the group&#39;s largest R&amp;D site with more than 700 employees who work daily to make steel a cutting-edge product. Coming from the best academic backgrounds, its community of researchers, made up of more than 35 nationalities, designs and industrializes new products for the automotive, packaging and construction markets. To meet the group&#39;s ambitions, the decarbonization of steel production is now one of the main drivers of research carried out on the site.</p>
			</div>

		</div>
	
		<div class="box">
			<h2>Description</h2>
			<div class="text">
				<p><u><strong>Reference Offre 140</strong></u></p>

<p>&nbsp;</p>

<p>The hot strip mill is the process where a hot-rolled coil is produced from a slab after being rolled multiple times in the rolling stands and then cooled with water using a run-out table to control the shape and the mechanical properties of the strip.<br />
A run-out table is key process in hot rolling composed of water jets controlled by multiple valves to reach a desired temperature along the length of the product. R&amp;D developed an industrial water-cooling controller for run-out table based on temperature and metallurgical predictions.<br />
<br />
Temperature control is a key challenge in such manufacturing processes, where product quality strongly depends on the ability to accurately predict and regulate thermal behaviour under a wide range of operating conditions.<br />
<br />
Current run out table control systems rely on physical and mathematical models to predict process behaviour and determine optimal operating parameters. While these models provide robust and accurate predictions in most situations, their performance may decrease when operating outside their calibration domain or when confronted with atypical process conditions.<br />
<br />
The objective of this internship is to investigate and develop a hybrid modelling approach combining physical models and neural networks to improve the adaptation capabilities of the run out table controller. The work will consist in leveraging historical process data and existing adaptation mechanisms to enhance prediction accuracy and controller performance while maintaining the robustness required for industrial deployment.<br />
<br />
The activities of the mission will include:<br />
<br />
- Understanding the industrial process and the existing control strategy.<br />
- Reviewing state-of-the-art machine learning approaches for industrial applications.<br />
- Analysing historical process and production data.<br />
- Developing and training neural network models in Python.<br />
- Reusing and extending existing adaptation algorithms.<br />
- Integrating machine learning models into the control adaptation workflow.<br />
- Evaluating model performance through offline simulations and industrial case studies.<br />
- Comparing conventional physical-model-based and hybrid AI-based approaches.<br />
- Documenting developments and presenting results to the R&amp;D team.<br />
<br />
The training will be provided by a team of technicians and engineers in Maizi&egrave;res lab. Some contacts with ArcelorMittal Hot Strip Mills could be requested to gather information.<br />
<br />
The language of everyday communication is in French but reports and presentations will be in English. The internship offers the opportunity to combine data science, software development and industrial process understanding on a real-world application.</p>
			</div>
			<div class="text">
							</div>
		</div>

		<div class="box">
			<h2>
				Profil			</h2>
			<div class="text">
				<p>Niveau d&#39;&eacute;tude: Bac+5 / Master<br />
Domaine: Computer Science / Software Development<br />
Data Science / AI / Image Processing<br />
Mathematics / Statistics<br />
Electronics / Mechatronics / Automation<br />
Modeling / Simulation<br />
Comp&eacute;tences: Python programming skills, Knowedge on Machine Learning and neural network, Analytical and<br />
problem-solving abilities, Ability to communicate technical concepts and present results in a<br />
multidisciplinary environment, Autonomous, curious and willing to learn.<br />
Langues: English, French</p>
			</div>

				Prise de fonction			</h3>
			<div class="text">
				Dès que possible			</div>
```
